### [1] [An approximate application of quantum gravity to the rotation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12461)
*R. Michael Jones*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证了量子引力通过路径积分中的相位干涉可以自然解决广义相对论中的“旋转问题”，即在宇宙早期（约大爆炸后0.25秒）将物质与惯性系的相对旋转速率压制到极低水平（<10^{-51} rad/year），且该结论对量子引力理论细节不敏感，主要依赖于可见宇宙大小、普朗克时间、光速、哈勃参数及暴胀e-fold数等基本参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论允许物质与惯性系之间存在任意大的相对旋转，但观测表明其旋转率极低，这一矛盾被称为“旋转问题”。本文旨在用量子引力解释为何观测到的相对旋转率如此之小。

Method: 采用量子引力的路径积分方法，将每条“路径”视为一个具有不同均方根相对旋转率的宇宙学，基于爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（包含大尺度相对旋转与物质分布），计算各宇宙学对路径积分的贡献权重，并通过相位干涉抑制高旋转率的贡献。

Result: 约大爆炸后0.25秒，量子宇宙学将显著贡献于路径积分的宇宙的相对旋转率压制到<10^{-51} rad/year（50 e-foldings暴胀）；55或60 e-foldings下旋转率更低；即使无暴胀，该时刻旋转率也<10^{-32} rad/year。作用量在零相对旋转率处取极值。

Conclusion: 量子引力通过相位干涉机制自然解决了旋转问题，且计算结果对量子引力理论的细节不敏感，仅依赖于少数基本宇宙学参数。

Abstract: Arbitrary initial conditions allow solutions of Einstein's field equations for General Relativity to have arbitrarily large relative rotation of matter and inertial frames. The ``Rotation Problem'' is to explain why the measured relative rotation rate is so small. Nearly any reasonable theory of quantum gravity can solve the rotation problem by phase interference. Even as early as about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity, quantum cosmology would limit the cosmologies that contribute significantly to a path integral calculation to have relative rms rotation rates less than about $10^{-51}$ rad/year. Those calculations are based on using 50 e-foldings during inflation. For 55 or 60 e-foldings, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have even smaller relative rotation rates. In addition, although inflation dominates the calculation, even if there had been no inflation, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have relative rotation rates less than about $10^{-32}$ rad/year at about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity. These calculations are insensitive to the details of the theory of quantum gravity because the main factor depends only on the size of the visible universe, the Planck time, the free-space speed of light, the Hubble parameter, and the number of e-foldings during inflation. These calculations use the Einstein-Hilbert action in quantum gravity, including large-scale relative rotation of inertial frames and the matter distribution, in which each ``path'' is a cosmology with a different rms relative rotation rate. The calculation shows that the action is an extremum at zero rms relative rotation rate.

</details>


### [2] [Black Hole Radiation Sparsity and Bekenstein Entropy Loss in Non-Commutative Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12470)
*Abdellah Touati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非对易引力理论框架下研究了史瓦西黑洞的辐射稀疏性、贝肯斯坦熵损失和总粒子发射。结果表明非对易修正消除了温度的发散行为，熵出现对数修正，总发射粒子数与熵行为成比例，且非对易史瓦西黑洞在蒸发最后阶段表现出极稀疏的辐射并趋于发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对易规范引力理论框架下，研究史瓦西黑洞的辐射稀疏性、贝肯斯坦熵损失以及总粒子发射，以探讨非对易几何对黑洞热力学和辐射特性的修正效应。

Method: 基于非对易规范引力理论，通过计算变形后的霍金温度和熵，分析贝肯斯坦熵损失和总发射粒子数的非对易修正，并利用稀疏性参数$\hatη$评估黑洞辐射的稀疏程度。

Result: 非对易修正消除了温度的发散行为，熵出现对数修正；总发射粒子数与NC SBH的熵行为成比例，符合非热辐射特征；NC SBH的辐射极度稀疏（$\hatη \gg 1$），且在黑洞蒸发最后阶段辐射稀疏性发散，黑洞停止辐射。

Conclusion: 非对易引力理论有效修正了史瓦西黑洞的经典热力学奇点问题，且非对易史瓦西黑洞的辐射具有高度稀疏性，在蒸发末期辐射完全停止，这为理解黑洞信息丢失问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the sparsity of black hole radiation, the Bekenstein entropy loss, and the total particle emission of a Schwarzschild black hole (SBH) within the framework of non-commutative (NC) gauge theory of gravity. First, we provide a brief review of black hole (BH) thermodynamics, computing both the deformed Hawking temperature and entropy. In this geometry, the divergent behavior of the temperature is removed, and a logarithmic correction to the entropy emerges. We then present the NC corrections to the Bekenstein entropy loss of the SBH alongside the total number of emitted particles. Our results show that the total number of emitted particles is proportional to the entropy behavior of the NC SBH, which is consistent with non-thermal radiation. Finally, we analyze the sparsity of Hawking radiation in this geometry, finding that the NC SBH exhibits extremely sparse radiation ($\hatη \gg 1$), which diverges at the final stage of evaporation as the black hole ceases to radiate.

</details>

*Federico Fantoccoli,Francisco Duque,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究LISA空间引力波探测器如何利用极端质量比旋近（EMRI）与吸积盘的相互作用来约束气体扭矩，发现基于完全贝叶斯框架可同时估算盘面密度和吸积率，且扭矩振幅可被约束在~10%精度内，无需电磁对应体即可探测亚微秒差距尺度的吸积物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LISA能否通过EMRI与吸积盘的相互作用来约束气体扭矩，以检验此前基于牛顿近似的预测，并评估其在不依赖电磁对应体的情况下探测强引力场区域吸积物理的可行性。

Method: 采用完全贝叶斯分析框架，结合近期发展的相对论性双星-盘相互作用模型，研究EMRI嵌入吸积盘时气体扭矩的可观测性，并与基于线性信号近似（Fisher矩阵）的简化方法进行对比。

Result: 1）可同时估算吸积盘面密度和吸积率（总光度）；2）线性信号近似在此系统中不适用；3）典型EMRI观测中，扭矩振幅约束精度可达~10%。

Conclusion: LISA有能力探测亚微秒差距尺度的吸积物理，补充电磁观测，有助于理解大质量黑洞的增长与演化，并通过与AGN星表交叉关联识别EMRI宿主星系，提升其在宇宙学中作为暗汽笛的应用价值。

Abstract: We study the ability of the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) to constrain gas torques acting on extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) when these are embedded in accretion disks, using recently developed relativistic models for the binary-disk interaction. Using a fully Bayesian setup, we find that, contrary to previous forecasts based on Newtonian results, these observations can provide simultaneous estimates of the disk surface density and the accretion rate (or, equivalently, its total luminosity) without the need for an electromagnetic counterpart. Our analysis also indicates that simpler measurement constraints based on the linear-signal (Fisher matrix) approximation are not valid for these systems. For typical EMRI observations, the torque amplitude can be constrained to within ~10%, strengthening the prospect of probing accretion physics at (sub)microparsec scales, deep in the strong-field gravity regime and complementing electromagnetic observations. This also strengthens LISA's ability to help answering questions such as how massive black holes grow and coevolve with their host galaxies and, by helping to identify the EMRI's host galaxy through cross-correlation with AGN catalogues, to improve the use of these sources as (dark) sirens for cosmology.

</details>


### [4] [Maxwell equations in Schwarzschild spacetime for static and freely falling observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12630)
*F. L. Carneiro,L. V. A. Cunha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在tetrad框架下构建了弯曲时空中的经典电动力学，将其应用于Schwarzschild时空的静态观测者和径向自由下落观测者，揭示了不同观测者所见的麦克斯韦方程结构差异，并通过有效介质类比解释了引力效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空中，电磁场的物理解释依赖于观测者的选择。现有研究往往缺乏对观测者依赖性的系统处理，该工作旨在建立一个基于tetrad的框架，保留电磁场直接的物理可解释性，并澄清不同观测者视角下引力对电磁场的影响方式。

Method: 采用tetrad（标架）形式化弯曲时空中的电动力学，保证电磁场分量具有直接的观测者意义。将该形式化应用于Schwarzschild度规，分别处理静态观测者和径向自由下落观测者两种情形，推导对应的麦克斯韦方程组，并分析弱场和近视界极限行为，最后通过有效介质类比进行讨论。

Result: 对静态观测者，麦克斯韦方程保持球对称结构，但引力通过度规函数在径向和时间方向引入几何修正；对自由下落观测者，局部径向boost引入运动学贡献，导致电荷密度与径向电流混合，电、磁扇区在时-径投影中相互交织，而角向安培-麦克斯韦和法拉第方程保持与静态观测者相同的形式。有效介质类比将Schwarzschild几何描述为静态观测者下的静止不均匀介质，以及自由下落观测者下的径向运动介质。

Conclusion: 该工作为弯曲时空电动力学提供了观测者依赖的系统框架，展示了不同观测者所见电磁场结构差异的物理来源，并通过有效介质类比为理解引力对电磁现象的影响提供了直观图像。

Abstract: Classical electrodynamics in curved spacetime is formulated within a tetrad-based framework that preserves the direct physical interpretation of the electromagnetic fields measured by an observer. The formalism is applied to Schwarzschild spacetime for two distinct families of observers described in the same coordinate system: static observers and radially free-falling observers. For static observers, Maxwell equations retain their usual spherical structure, while the gravitational field introduces geometrical corrections in the radial and temporal sectors through the metric function. These corrections are interpreted in terms of proper radial distance, proper time, and the radial variation of the lapse function. For radially free-falling observers, additional kinematical contributions arise from the local radial boost relating the free-falling frame to the static one. As a consequence, charge density mixes with radial current, and the electric and magnetic sectors become intertwined in the temporal-radial projections of Maxwell equations, whereas the angular Ampère-Maxwell and Faraday equations retain the same structure found for the static observer. The weak-field and near-horizon regimes are also examined, and the results are discussed through an effective-medium analogy, in which the Schwarzschild geometry behaves as an inhomogeneous geometrical medium at rest for static observers and as a radially moving effective medium in the temporal-radial sector for free-falling observers.

</details>


### [5] [Higher Dimensional Loop Quantum Black hole in de Sitter Spacetime: Quasinormal Modes and Shadow Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12698)
*Kourosh Nozari,Sara Saghafi,Ali Mohammadpour*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了德西特背景下高维圈量子修正黑洞的动力学和光学性质，通过三种方法计算标量扰动的准正则模，发现圈量子修正对准正则谱影响较小而时空维度影响显著；同时分析了零测地线和黑洞阴影，利用M87*的EHT观测数据约束了模型参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究圈量子引力效应和高维时空结构在强引力区域中的可观测特征，为准正则模和黑洞阴影观测提供互补的检验手段。

Method: 使用时域演化结合Prony提取、矩阵法和WKB近似三种方法计算准正则模；通过零测地线分析构建黑洞阴影，并与Event Horizon Telescope对M87*的观测数据对比约束参数空间。

Result: 圈量子修正引起准正则谱的中等程度偏移，而时空维度增加导致振荡频率和阻尼率显著升高；所有模式的负虚部表明在探索参数范围内系统动力学稳定；额外维度总体上减小阴影尺寸。

Conclusion: 准正则模和黑洞阴影观测可作为圈量子引力效应和高维时空结构在强引力区的互补探针。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical and optical properties of a higher-dimensional loop-quantum-corrected black hole in a de Sitter background. The quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations are computed using time-domain evolution with Prony extraction, the matrix method, and the WKB approximation, showing good agreement among the three approaches. We find that loop quantum corrections induce moderate shifts in the quasinormal spectrum, whereas the spacetime dimensionality has a much stronger impact, leading to higher oscillation frequencies and damping rates. The negative imaginary parts of all modes indicate dynamical stability against massless scalar perturbations within the explored parameter range. We also analyze null geodesics and construct the corresponding black hole shadow. The shadow radius depends sensitively on the loop quantum parameter, the cosmological constant, and the number of spacetime dimensions, with extra dimensions generally reducing the shadow size. By comparing the theoretical shadow radius with the Event Horizon Telescope constraints for M87$^{\ast}$, we obtain bounds on the parameter space of the model. These results suggest that quasinormal modes and black hole shadow observables can provide complementary probes of loop quantum gravity effects and higher-dimensional spacetime structure in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [6] [Asymmetric quantum steering harvested near a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12766)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在洛伦兹破坏的BTZ黑洞时空中，两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器之间的量子导引及其方向不对称性被研究。结果表明，受更强有效热噪声的探测器反而表现出更强的导引能力，揭示了弯曲时空中热直觉的反转现象；量子导引仅在有限能量间隙窗口内存在，并在最优区间达到最大；洛伦兹破坏在最优能量间隙附近最强地抑制导引，表明对称性破缺对最大关联提取的增强敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弯曲时空（特别是洛伦兹破坏的BTZ黑洞）中量子导引的 harvesting 行为及其方向不对称性，理解引力效应（如引力红移）和洛伦兹破坏对量子关联的影响。

Method: 采用两个位于黑洞外不同径向位置的Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，考虑洛伦兹破坏的BTZ黑洞背景，研究探测器间量子导引的 harvest 及其不对称性，分析有效温度、能隙和洛伦兹破坏参数的影响。

Result: 发现两个反直觉结果：(1) 经历更高有效温度的探测器反而具有更强的导引能力，违反常规热直觉；(2) 洛伦兹破坏在最优能量间隙附近对导引的抑制最强。此外，量子导引仅在探测器能量间隙的有限窗口内存在。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破坏作为时空的几何约束，同时限制了量子关联的强度和方向性，揭示了对称性破缺对量子信息容量调控的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of quantum steering and its directional asymmetry between two Unruh-DeWitt detectors in a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole spacetime. Since the detectors are located at different radial positions outside the black hole, they experience inequivalent local environments induced by gravitational redshift, causing Alice to undergo stronger effective thermal noise than Bob. Remarkably, we uncover a counterintuitive phenomenon in which the detector subjected to a higher effective temperature exhibits stronger steerability than the other one, revealing a nontrivial inversion of thermal intuition in curved spacetime. Furthermore, quantum steering survives only within a finite window of detector energy gaps and reaches its maximum within an optimal regime. We find that Lorentz violation suppresses steering most strongly near this optimal energy gap, indicating an enhanced sensitivity of maximal correlation extraction to symmetry breaking effects. Our results demonstrate that Lorentz violation acts as a geometric constraint on the quantum information capacity of spacetime, simultaneously restricting both the strength and the directionality of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [7] [Constraining Kerr supermassive black hole properties using gravitational waves from inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12784)
*Jie Wu,Jin-Tao Yao,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究未来空间引力波探测器通过观测围绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）旋进的恒星级双黑洞（BBH）系统，来约束SMBH属性的能力。基于LISA配置和二阶时间延迟干涉技术，使用Fisher信息矩阵估计参数不确定性。结果显示，外轨道半长轴对参数精度影响最大，SMBH质量和轨道参数测量相对不确定度可达10^{-5}量级，自旋幅度和方向可约束到百分之几以内。对于M87*类系统，引力波观测比当前电磁观测能更精确地测量SMBH质量和自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用未来空间引力波探测器观测围绕SMBH旋进的恒星级BBH系统，以高精度约束SMBH属性（质量、自旋等）——这是当前电磁观测手段难以实现的目标。

Method: 采用稳定层级三体系统模型，在Kerr时空中描述BBH运动，使用后牛顿波形结合移动源变换计算调制的引力波信号；基于LISA配置和二阶时间延迟干涉技术，通过Fisher信息矩阵估计参数不确定性。

Result: 外轨道半长轴对参数精度影响最强；高信噪比信号下，SMBH质量和轨道参数相对不确定度达10^{-5}量级，自旋幅度和方向可约束到百分之几以内；对于M87*类系统，引力波测量精度优于电磁观测。

Conclusion: 空间引力波天文学具有以高精度探测SMBH属性的巨大潜力，能够提供比当前电磁观测更精确的SMBH质量和自旋测量。

Abstract: We study the capability of future space-based gravitational-wave (GW) detectors to constrain supermassive black hole (SMBH) properties through observations of inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) orbiting them. Focusing on stable hierarchical triple systems, we model the BBH motion in Kerr spacetime and compute the modulated GW signals using the post Newtonian waveform combined with moving-source transformation. Based on the LISA configuration and second-generation time delay interferometry technology, we estimate parameter uncertainties with the Fisher information matrix. Our results show that the outer semimajor axis has the strongest influence on parameter precision, while the SMBH spin and eccentricity mainly affect their own uncertainties. For high-SNR signals, the SMBH mass and orbital parameters can be measured with relative uncertainties on the order of $10^{-5}$, while the spin magnitude and its orientation can be constrained to within a few percentages. Applying the method to an M87*-like system, GW observations provide more precise measurements of the SMBH mass and spin compared with current electromagnetic observations, highlighting the potential of space-based GW astronomy to probe SMBH properties with high accuracy.

</details>


### [8] [Strong deflection limit-analysis using Picard-Fuchs equation in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12819)
*Tadashi Sasaki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿理论中带电球对称黑洞对光的偏折，利用Picard-Fuchs方程和Painlevé VI方程，通过强偏折极限下的对数公式，唯一确定了零电荷极限下与Schwarzschild黑洞一致的偏折系数$\bar{a}$和$\bar{b}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电黑洞（特别是Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton理论中的黑洞）对光的偏折效应，该效应可通过椭圆积分表示。作者希望建立偏折角与相关变量之间的精确数学描述，并求解强偏折极限下的对数公式中的常数。

Method: 首先将偏折角表示为两个无量纲变量$(s,z)$的函数，并证明其满足二阶线性偏微分方程组（Picard-Fuchs方程）。对于每种狄拉顿耦合情形，PF方程导出关于变量$s$的一阶常微分方程，用于强偏折极限对数公式中的常数$\bar{a}$和$\bar{b}$。利用与Painlevé VI方程相关的哈密顿系统（由PF方程的可积性导出）求解$\bar{a}$和$\bar{b}$，并通过零电荷极限下与Schwarzschild情形的一致性确定非零电荷下的唯一解。

Result: 成功建立了偏折角满足Picard-Fuchs方程的理论框架，并利用Painlevé VI方程的哈密顿系统求解了强偏折极限下的常数$\bar{a}$和$\bar{b}$，在零电荷极限下与Schwarzschild黑洞结果一致，得到了非零电荷情形的唯一确定值。

Abstract: We consider the deflection of light by a spherically symmetric and electrically charged black hole solution in the Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton theory with specific values of the dilaton coupling constant where the deflection angle can be represented as elliptic integrals. Abstract: This paper develops an epistemological constraint on quantum gravity grounded in the empirical meaning of general relativity. In classical general relativity, they are usually implicit in the use of clocks, rods, light signals, freely falling bodies, detectors, and gauge-invariant observables. In quantum gravity, however, they become non-trivial because spacetime geometry may be emergent, effective, thermodynamic, relational, or frame-dependent. This claim is developed through four cases: Rindler horizons and the Unruh effect, black-hole thermodynamics and Jacobson's equation-of-state derivation, gravitational-wave detection, and Weyl and conformal gravity. Implications for quantum gravity are also discussed using emergent gravity and quantum reference frames as examples. Abstract: We investigate the construction of regular compact objects in the recently proposed lower-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet (EGB) gravity obtained through regularized dimensional reduction. Unlike the standard BTZ black hole, the corresponding vacuum EGB solution develops a genuine curvature singularity at the origin, providing an interesting setting in which higher-curvature corrections deteriorate the ultraviolet behavior of spacetime. We then extend the analysis to Simpson--Visser black-bounce geometries, obtaining smooth throat configurations with finite curvature invariants throughout the spacetime. As an alternative regularization mechanism, we formulate a unimodular extension of lower-dimensional EGB gravity and show that standard Maxwell fields can support regular geometries through a dynamical exchange between the vacuum and matter sectors mediated by a spacetime-dependent cosmological function. We further investigate the thermodynamic properties of the regular black-hole and black-bounce solutions, showing that the matter sector modifies the evaporation process, allows for remnant formation, and produces nontrivial phase transitions. In the black-bounce case, the thermodynamic quantities smoothly recover the EGB-BTZ behavior in the appropriate limit. These results demonstrate that lower-dimensional EGB gravity provides a useful laboratory for exploring the interplay between higher-curvature corrections, regular compact objects, nonlinear electrodynamics, and unimodular gravity.

</details>

Abstract: We develop an affine-null characteristic formulation of the Einstein-Maxwell system coupled to a charged complex scalar field in $n$-dimensional spherical symmetry. As a consistency check, we recover the scalar-free Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini family, including both the non-extremal and extremal branches, directly from the hierarchy. Abstract: The detection of high-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) above 10 kHz provides a crucial probe of exotic astrophysical phenomena and new physics. We report the first search for HFGWs via their conversion to electromagnetic radiation through the inverse Gertsenshtein effect in Earth's magnetic field, utilizing radio telescopes including the Very Large Array (VLA) and the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA). Since no statistically significant signal is observed, we obtain new upper limits on the characteristic strain across the 1 GHz -- 1 THz band, with the most stringent constraint reaching $h_c \lesssim 10^{-18}$, improving upon existing bounds by up to three orders of magnitude. These results significantly advance the exploration of uncharted parameter space for exotic gravitational-wave sources, paving the way for future discoveries with next-generation facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array (SKA).

*Lin-Qing Chen,Flaminia Giacomini*

Method: 在线性化量子引力框架下，引入四个动力学标量场作为量子参考场，其应力-能量张量进入引力约束方程。推广量子参考系的中性视角构造方法，构建规范不变的关系性可观测量，推导不同量子参考场视角之间的幺正变换，并建立关系性von Neumann测量方案。

Abstract: Diffeomorphism invariance is a central feature of general relativity. Without external reference structures, matter and geometry must be specified relationally, with respect to internal subsystems serving as reference frames. In quantum gravity, these reference systems must themselves be treated as quantum, motivating the use of quantum reference frames. In this work, we address how such a relational description could be formulated within linearized quantum gravity. To this purpose, we introduce quantum reference fields, i.e. sets of four dynamical scalar fields whose stress-energy tensors enter the gravitational constraints. These fields extend the notion of quantum reference frames to local field-theoretic reference systems, allowing matter and gravitational degrees of freedom to be described relationally with respect to physical quantum systems. By generalizing the perspective-neutral construction of quantum reference frames, we show that relational, gauge invariant observables admit reduced descriptions in the perspective of each quantum reference field, and we derive the unitary transformations relating them. The resulting unitary maps implement local quantum coordinate changes between different internal perspectives, and act on the linearized gravitational field with an analogous structure to a linearized diffeomorphism, but with the classical gauge parameter replaced by a physical quantum field. Finally, we construct a relational von Neumann-type measurement scheme, showing how the corresponding reduced observables can be accessed operationally from the perspective of a quantum reference field.

</details>

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 针对EnKF在含间断解（如激波）中失效的问题，提出一种特征保持的潜在-EnKF方法，通过在学习的低维潜在空间中进行集合更新，避免伪振荡，数值实验验证了其对激波和接触间断的准确恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EnKF在含激波等间断的可压缩流中失效，因为不确定性导致的激波位置变化使集合统计呈多峰分布，违反高斯假设，从而在分析状态中产生大尺度伪振荡。

Method: 提出特征保持的潜在-EnKF：1) 将集合成员编码到学习的低维潜在空间，其中激波和流动特征呈现光滑流形表示；2) 在潜在空间中进行EnKF更新；3) 通过共享解码器将更新后的潜在状态映射回物理状态。该方法消除了先前方法中成员特定的有序训练和正数下限处理。

Result: 在Sod激波管和Mach 2激波与二维圆柱相互作用的数值实验中，使用稀疏噪声观测，该方法能够准确恢复激波和接触间断，且无伪振荡。

Conclusion: 潜在空间中的EnKF更新能有效保持激波等间断特征，为解决含间断问题的数据同化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF) is widely adopted for sequential data assimilation, but fails for solutions with discontinuities, such as shocks in compressible flows. Uncertainty in shock location induces multimodal ensemble statistics that violate the Gaussian assumptions underlying the EnKF, producing large-scale spurious oscillations in the analysis state. We introduce a feature-preserving latent-EnKF that performs the ensemble update in a learned low-dimensional latent space, where shock and flow features admit a smooth manifold representation, thereby preserving sharp features during EnKF analysis. The updated latent state is mapped back to physical state through a shared decoder for all ensemble members. The algorithm eliminates the member-specific ordered training and positivity flooring used in prior approaches. Numerical experiments on a Sod shock tube and Mach 2 shock interaction with a 2D cylinder, using sparse and noisy observations, show accurate feature recovery of shocks and contact discontinuities without spurious oscillations.

</details>


### [23] [REMAL: Residual Equilibrium Manifold Active Learning for Surrogate-Based Multidisciplinary Design Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13245)
*Kail Yuan,Ashwin Renganathan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: REMAL是一种基于残差流形代理建模的框架，通过多任务高斯过程模型学习耦合系统的联合残差流形，并采用熵驱动的主动学习策略，实现对耦合系统平衡态的高效预测，在多个工程基准案例中表现出显著的计算成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的固定点迭代在每个设计点分别求解耦合系统的一致性条件，当学科评估成本高昂且需要进行大量外环任务（如多学科设计优化、不确定性量化或数字孪生更新）时，计算开销巨大。现有替代方法要么独立近似各学科，要么直接学习收敛后的耦合变量，均存在局限性。

Method: REMAL框架的核心是：(1) 通过多任务高斯过程模型构建联合残差流形的代理模型；(2) 采用基于熵的主动学习策略，在不确定的零等高线区域附近选择额外的残差评估点；(3) 对于新的设计输入，仅利用训练好的代理模型求解非线性最小二乘优化问题来恢复平衡态。

Result: 在四个工程耦合系统基准测试（卫星模型、气动结构模型、燃气轮机传热与经济学有限元模型、带反馈耦合的改进涡轮模型）上，REMAL在需要跨设计空间重复评估固定点的场景中，一致展现出成本效益优势。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明，在温和假设下，REMAL的预测固定点误差是有界的，这为其在实际工程应用中的可靠性提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Multidisciplinary design analysis of coupled engineering systems requires the computation of equilibrium states in which all disciplinary coupling variables are mutually consistent. Conventional fixed-point iteration resolves this consistency problem separately at each design point, which can become expensive when disciplinary evaluations are costly and many analyses are required in outer-loop tasks such as multidisciplinary design optimization, uncertainty quantification, or digital twin updating. This paper introduces REMAL, a residual manifold surrogate modeling framework for coupled systems. Instead of approximating each discipline independently or directly learning converged coupling variables, the proposed method learns a surrogate model of the joint residual manifold via multitask Gaussian process models. An entropy-based active learning strategy selects additional residual evaluations near uncertain zero-contour regions, and equilibrium states for new design inputs are recovered by solving a nonlinear least squares optimization problem using only the trained surrogate. The method is evaluated on four engineering coupled system benchmarks: a satellite model, an aerostructural model, a finite-element gas-turbine heat-transfer and economics model, and a modified turbine model with added feedback coupling. Across these cases, REMAL consistently demonstrates the cost effectiveness when repeated evaluations of the fixed point across the design space are necessary. Theoretically, we show that, under mild assumptions, REMAL's predictive fixed point error is bounded.

</details>

*Igor Itkin*

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测重新定义为变化点检测问题，建立了基于马尔可夫链的理论框架，验证了CUSUM检测器的优势，并揭示了基于token的分类评估无法反映的延迟结构问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的token级幻觉检测器使用AUC等分类指标进行评估，但这些指标无法反映流式监控中关键的检测延迟（从幻觉发生到报警的token数）。作者希望建立更符合实际应用场景的评估框架。

Method: 1) 将幻觉起始检测形式化为最快变化点检测问题；2) 基于RAGTruth数据验证了潜在状态（忠实/幻觉）的一阶马尔可夫模型；3) 应用Lorden下界理论；4) 证明因果循环标注器等价于CUSUM（累积和）检测器；5) 使用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理分析差距来源。

Result: 1) Lorden下界表明在虚警率0.01时，最优检测延迟约1.3个token；2) 因果循环标注器在匹配虚警率下检测延迟为11-13个token，优于线性per-token基线的31个token；3) 优势主要来自更好的per-token分数（而非时间累积）；4) 学习到的分数仅实现了特征所携带散度的1/4.5，这是不可通过重校准消除的差距。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了延迟结构，而序列分析使其变得可量化。当前检测器与理论下界之间存在数量级差距，主要源于特征散度利用率不足和有限时域效应。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>

*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Boltzmann注意力机制，通过引入可学习的成对耦合（Ising模型）来显式建模注意力头内位置间的交互作用，实验表明该方法在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准softmax注意力，且序列越长优势越明显。此外，该Ising公式化支持用量子退火进行采样训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算单个位置的相关性，虽经softmax归一化引入了位置间的竞争，但并未显式参数化注意力决策之间的可学习交互，因此无法直接建模注意力机制内部的协作或对抗性共注意结构。

Method: 将注意力模式建模为交互Ising模型：在标准数据相关的局部场基础上，增加可学习的成对耦合项，从而捕捉位置间相关性，扩展了softmax/sigmoid注意力的表达能力。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务中，Boltzmann注意力在标准Transformer架构上一致优于softmax注意力，且序列越长优势越明显；四路消融实验证实改进来自可学习的成对耦合。

Conclusion: 显式的跨位置交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了有原则的增强；Ising公式化还自然通向基于量子计算的采样策略，实验展示非绝热量子退火在保持与精确Boltzmann计算相当性能的同时提供了实用的训练方法。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [26] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过分层奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决强化学习驱动的LLM路由中奖励信号混杂和偏置问题，在7个数据集上稳定提升路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的LLM路由方法在异质任务下因多目标聚合为单一标量奖励导致信用分配模糊，且奖励信号在不同实例间差异大，引入偏向简单样本的优化偏差，需要更细粒度的学习信号和公正的优化策略。

Method: 1) 分层奖励分解机制，包含组件级优势估计；2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和逐数据集归一化校准优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上，ReCal在路由性能和训练稳定性方面持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过奖励分解和分布感知校准可有效缓解多目标冲突和样本偏差问题，提升基于RL的LLM路由效果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [27] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列表示为结构化符号程序，使LLM无需微调即可进行有效的时间序列推理，在编辑、标注和问答三个任务上优于原始序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列与LLM的文本模态存在不匹配，现有方法（如序列化数值或微调）将提取时间结构的负担直接放在模型上，导致长序列性能下降和计算开销大。需要一种更有效的表示方式，使LLM能够利用其已有的推理能力进行时间序列分析。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化符号程序的形式表达。这种表示与LLM预训练所基于的文本和代码模态对齐，将时间结构提取从模型转移到表示本身。

Result: 在编辑、标注和问答三个推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示一致地提升了性能，减少了推理时间，降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列和LLM之间提供了一种有效的接口，使LLM无需微调即可进行时间序列理解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [28] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数）方法，通过基于查询点与支持点间平方距离的简化计算，解决了传统Christoffel函数因矩阵求逆随维度指数增长而无法用于高维数据的可扩展性问题，在ADBench基准上平均精度超越14个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel函数方法在异常检测中具有坚实的数学基础和计算经济性，但需对维度指数增长的矩阵求逆，导致中高维数据不可行。本文旨在解决这一维度可扩展性瓶颈，同时保留其关键理论性质（开-关支撑二象性与精确支撑形状捕获）。

Method: 提出UCF，一种基于查询点与支持点间平方距离的单变量Christoffel函数，避免了高维矩阵求逆操作。

Result: 在ADBench基准上，UCF在平均精度上持续优于14个最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: UCF解决了Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，为异常检测工具箱增添了一种鲁棒、理论扎实且普适可用的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [29] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC)，一种分支级模仿学习框架，通过固定视野分支审查和回滚机制，在交互式Web代理训练中平衡专家干预时机，避免早期错误累积和过度依赖专家策略，在WebArena-Infinity上收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于专家轨迹的交互式Web代理模仿学习中，专家干预时机是关键挑战：延迟干预导致早期错误累积至不可恢复状态，过早或过度干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: SRC是一种分支级模仿框架，核心是固定视野分支审查（fixed-horizon branch review）：学生先执行短推测性分支（speculative segment），仅在局部进度停滞时教师才定位首个有害偏差；回滚保留有用前缀，成功轨迹通过硬验证器过滤并存入轻量级质量-多样性存档（quality-diversity archive）。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例；固定视野审查在恢复效果与查询次数之间的权衡上优于单步级审查，同时保留了通过验证器的多种解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级审查与回滚机制，在专家干预时机上实现了更好的权衡，既避免了早期错误累积，又防止了过度依赖专家策略，并有效收集了多样化的成功轨迹用于后续监督微调。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [30] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件预测性维护（PdM）的方法论，假设被监测组件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，通过选取最近观测数据并转换为表格格式，利用AutoML进行机器学习分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比现有SOTA方法降低了成本并简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的条件预测性维护（PdM）虽能减少停机时间和成本，但面临数据量大、故障检测复杂及实现成本权衡困难等挑战，需要更有效且成本更低的方法。

Method: 假设组件磨损状态为单调非递减时间序列；仅选取时间序列中最新的观测值；将其转换为表格格式；使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习模型进行分类；通过AutoML简化建模过程。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提方法相比当前SOTA方法降低了成本。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效降低预测性维护成本，并通过AutoML简化建模流程，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [31] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中量化敏感层并仅对这些层使用8位激活精度（其余保持4位），解决了W4A4KV4量化中激活值剧烈波动导致的跨层残差动态不稳定问题，在LLaMA-2/3上提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，并实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法虽然通过正交旋转和仿射缩放等变换基元来缓解极端激活值问题，但忽视了残差流的跨层动态特性：大规模激活值在网络深度上以阶段式模式出现和消失，引发大幅残差变化，导致新注入的逐层更新主导4位量化尺度并削弱历史残差信息。静态变换基元无法完全解决这种动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标来表征跨层残差动态行为；基于此提出DynamicPTQ，这是一个阶段感知混合精度激活量化策略，从残差流动态中识别量化敏感层，仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，而权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持4位；可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等PTQ基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升，且内存开销适度。

Conclusion: DynamicPTQ为实现鲁棒的低比特大模型推理提供了一条实用路径，证明了通过动态识别并混合精度处理量化敏感层可以有效克服跨层残差动态带来的量化不稳定问题。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [32] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然具备兼容性，并提出通过交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合来捕捉高阶依赖关系，在模型持续更新场景下实现了无需重新处理图库图像的最优检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型持续更新或替换时，需要学习可兼容的特征表示，使得新旧模型提取的特征可以互换使用，从而避免重复处理已有数据。

Method: 1. 理论证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示满足兼容性的形式化定义；2. 提出交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合训练方法，在保持兼容性约束的同时捕捉高阶依赖关系；3. 在顺序微调和模型替换的新场景下进行实验验证。

Result: 所提方法在模型更新和替换时无需重新处理图库图像即可实现不间断的检索服务，性能达到最优水平。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器为学习兼容表示提供了理论基础，结合对比度损失能更好地捕获高阶统计依赖，适用于实际中模型持续更新的检索场景。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [33] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RNN认证中由于非线性松弛导致近似误差累积的问题，提出了一种抽象-精炼框架，通过区间划分去除主导松弛误差，并引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，在CIFAR10和MNIST基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性认证因非线性松弛引入的近似误差通过循环连接随时间累积而极具挑战性，现有的线性边界传播方法过于保守，尤其在预激活区间过零时难以认证实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象-精炼框架：1）划分过零的预激活区间以消除松弛误差，使ReLU精确化、tanh/sigmoid获得更紧的线性包络；2）引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按对认证目标的贡献度排序隐状态，仅精炼最关键的时序步。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST笔画基准上，相比纯抽象基线方法，认证成功率和鲁棒性边界紧度均有持续提升，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型间明确的运行时间权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象-精炼框架能有效缓解RNN认证中松弛误差累积问题，SHAP引导的时间步选择策略在控制组合爆炸成本的同时提升了认证性能。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [34] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev²，一种联合事件语义与网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，通过结构引导的掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程模拟事件在网络中的传播演化，并配套发布了多模态基准数据集Net-Ev²-6.5M和新拓扑保真度评估指标JL-MMD，实验表明其达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少真实世界的试错成本是决策制定的核心目标，而模拟干扰事件（如事故）在网络中的影响传播比单纯建模未来状态更具挑战性。现有方法无法同时处理事件的结构化属性和非结构化语义，也无法捕捉网络拓扑结构来模拟事件的演化过程。

Method: Net-Ev²包含两个阶段：1) 结构引导的掩码预训练；2) 拓扑感知的扩散过程，其中通过U-Net风格的图下采样和上采样实现去噪。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模道路网络上的实验表明Net-Ev²达到最先进性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²成功将事件语义与网络拓扑结构联合建模，为网络事件演化模拟提供了有效的生成式框架，并提供了配套基准数据集和评估指标。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [35] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，在强预训练VLA骨干网络中仅添加少量可学习记忆token（通过自注意力跨时间步更新），研究了循环机制对部分可观测任务的作用。μVLA在MIKASA-Robo上训练任务成功率从0.42提升至0.84，LIBERO上达96.2%无回归，表明最小化循环机制在特定场景下足够，但不同记忆结构需求的任务中性能无提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构变化，导致循环机制本身的贡献被其他模块混淆，缺乏对循环机制在VLA中的独立作用进行受控隔离研究。

Method: 在预训练VLA Transformer中仅添加少量可学习记忆token，通过自注意力跨时间步更新，使用截断反向传播时间（TBPTT）端到端训练，无辅助损失和架构变化。定义μVLA系列，参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或分离EMA），使循环成为唯一变量。

Result: MIKASA-Robo上训练任务成功率从0.42提升至0.84（最强设置），保留任务达0.23（基线0.07）；不同记忆结构需求任务性能接近基线。LIBERO上最强循环变体达96.2%平均成功率，完全可观测下无回归。

Conclusion: 最小化骨干网络内循环机制在部分可观测任务中有效，但存在能力边界：在特定记忆结构需求下足够，而在需要不同记忆结构的场景中仍需额外记忆架构。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [36] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了基于规则的驾驶行为模型与机器学习（ML）模型在交通微观仿真中的表现，发现ML模型生成的冲突数据能通过极值理论准确预测真实事故频率，而规则模型因缺乏校准无法提供有效预测；但直接使用ML模型生成的事故数据预测效果不佳，说明当前ML模型能真实再现冲突但尚无法生成真实事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微观仿真的安全研究多采用简化的规则型驾驶行为模型，虽能较好再现交通流，但难以生成真实的冲突动态，限制了事故预测精度。机器学习模型从大规模轨迹数据中学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性和事故预测能力，值得系统验证。

Method: 以英国利兹市五个真实信号交叉口为场景，分别使用标准规则模型和最新ML模型进行交通微观仿真；采用二维Time-to-Collision指标识别仿真冲突，并利用极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）建模以预测事故频率。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突数据得出的事故预测与真实事故数据吻合，而规则模型因缺乏针对具体交叉口的校准无法产生有意义预测；直接使用ML模型生成的仿真事故来预测真实事故频率效果不佳。

Conclusion: ML驾驶行为模型能通过仿真冲突有效提升事故预测精度，且无需针对特定地点进行模型校准；当前ML模型可真实再现冲突但尚不能直接生成真实事故，未来研究应聚焦于ML模型在微观仿真安全评估中的进一步改进。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>

*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

TL;DR: Conformal QoT是一个结合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略的框架，在领域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光路质量（QoT）估计方法在领域偏移（domain shift）下预测可靠性不足，需要一种能够提供统计保证且适应环境变化的框架。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，采用策略驱动的方式，将符合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合。

Result: 在开放数据集上，该框架将光路可行性预测的准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够在领域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，显著提升预测准确率。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [38] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dolph2Vec，首个针对海豚通信的大规模物种特异性自监督学习模型，在签名哨声分类和检测任务上显著优于通用基线，且学到的嵌入和码本结构能捕捉可解释的声学单元，用于细粒度通信模式分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物声学领域的自监督模型偏向跨物种泛化，未能优化以揭示个体通信系统的细粒度结构，缺乏针对特定物种的专用SSL模型。

Method: 收集了超过五年纵贯记录的5只已知海豚数据集，适配Wav2Vec2.0架构，训练了首个大规模物种特异性SSL模型Dolph2Vec。

Result: 在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两项生物相关任务中，Dolph2Vec显著优于通用基线模型；其嵌入和码本结构可捕捉与海豚哨声类别及亚哨声结构对应的可解释声学单元。

Conclusion: 自监督学习可以作为模型和科学工具，用于探索动物通信研究中的假设，实现细粒度的通信模式分析。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [39] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPOP方法，通过在DPO损失中引入对参考模型贪婪响应的门控惩罚，利用被忽略的参考模型信号来改进偏好优化，在AlpacaEval 2.0上超越DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）仅使用数据集中已选和已拒响应，忽略了参考模型自身生成的响应这一有用信号。

Method: 在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项，仅当当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍低于对拒绝响应的似然时激活该惩罚，惩罚针对参考模型的贪婪（greedy）响应。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比基线方法提升5.3%和4.4%的length-controlled win rate。

Conclusion: DPOP有效利用了参考模型信号；消融实验表明SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚在该设置下优于NPO和token-level unlikelihood。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [40] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGSD，一种无验证器的训练方法，通过将基于规则的提示条件策略（教师）蒸馏到无条件策略（学生），实现逐token的密集学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，在医疗和科学领域多个模型上达到与基于评判器的GRPO相当的规则满足度，同时无需训练时验证器调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于规则的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对每个轨迹评分，导致训练开销大、优化受验证器偏见影响、规则反馈稀疏（仅在轨迹末端）。为解决这些问题，需要一种无需验证器的、提供密集信号的高效训练方法。

Method: RGSD（规则引导自蒸馏）：将基础策略作为教师（输入规则作为条件），未条件化的策略作为学生，通过逐token蒸馏将教师分布传递给学生，以密集的逐token学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，完全移除训练循环中的LLM评判器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) 和 Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) 模型上，医疗和科学领域实验中，RGSD达到与基于评判器的GRPO相当的规则满足度，仅使用每个提示一次在线 rollout，无需训练时验证器调用。消融实验表明原始规则比自生成参考响应提供更强的教师信号。

Conclusion: RGSD是一种有效的无验证器替代方案，在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时尤为适用。更强GRPO评判器在部分设置下仍可超越RGSD，说明RGSD作为互补方法而非替代方案。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [41] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过交叉验证的多折划分来提升机器学习性能评估的置信度，引入"样本增益"概念量化虚拟数据增强效果，并开发动态早停策略以提高评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域存在验证危机：由于测试样本有限和算法随机性，性能估计往往不可靠，难以区分真正的算法进步与统计波动。

Method: 引入"样本增益"概念量化多折交叉验证带来的虚拟数据增强效果；提出动态早停策略，通过前几折的结果预估后续折是否带来显著样本增益。

Result: 在合成数据、组织病理学扫描和NLP微调等真实数据集上，多折交叉验证显著提升了性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且收益递减点比预期更晚出现。

Conclusion: 强调充分利用交叉验证对现有样本进行多次划分，能够实现更稳健可靠的基准测试评估。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [42] [Emerging Flexible Designs for Geospatial Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12595)
*Philipe Dias,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Aristeidis Tsaris,Dan Lu,Xiao Wang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种地球观测基础模型架构（encoder-only、encoder-decoder、掩码自编码等），在统一的自监督学习目标、训练数据集和参数化条件下，基于GEOBench基准进行分类与分割任务评估，揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游性能之间的设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型架构多样性（encoder-only到encoder-decoder及掩码自编码范式）使得在一致条件下评估其性能权衡变得困难，缺乏公平的"苹果对苹果"比较。

Method: 采用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集进行标准化预训练，在GEOBench基准上以一致参数化设置评估所有模型在分类和分割任务上的表现。

Result: 揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间的设计权衡，明确了不同架构在受控条件下的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建具备鲁棒多模态推理能力的下一代地球观测基础模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 蛋白质语言模型在不平衡数据上训练，在嵌入空间中形成一条“自然性”轴，将细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白到随机序列排序。病毒蛋白虽被认为“不自然”，但其嵌入仍保留可线性分离的特异性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究蛋白质语言模型在不平衡数据集（病毒蛋白高度欠表示）下如何表征这些欠表示的生物序列。

Method: 以病毒蛋白为案例，利用ESM模型家族，分析嵌入空间中的“自然性”轴（与掩码重建困惑度对齐），并通过线性可分性分析评估病毒特异性信息的保留程度。

Result: 存在一条主导的“自然性”轴，将细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白、打乱序列和随机序列依次排序；模型缩放对该轴的收缩效应在不同病毒家族中不均匀；病毒蛋白的嵌入在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外仍保持线性可分性。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型的表示结构由广义的“自然性”概念组织，同时保留了不同生物组别特异的信息。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [44] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个补充性论点：AI历史上两次寒冬的深层原因不仅在于工程失败和商业失望，更在于当时主导范式遇到了表征限制、优化困难、计算复杂性、统计可学习性和高维逼近等正式的数学瓶颈，并通过几个经典理论结果加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常将AI寒冬归因于工程失败和商业失望，本文旨在补充一个正式的理论视角，证明早期AI的困境也与严格的形式化数学瓶颈密切相关。

Method: 采用综述性分析方法，综合梳理Minsky-Papert感知机不可能结果、Blum-Rivest的神经网络训练复杂性、Stone的高维非参数估计极小化收敛率、Hochreiter和Bengio等的梯度消失分析，以及Vapnik-Chervonenkis等人的统计学习理论，将这些理论瓶颈与AI寒冬中的具体失望事件建立对应。

Result: 展示了早期AI的多项核心失望与数学上精确的瓶颈是一致的，这些瓶颈包括表征局限性、计算复杂度的不可行性、高维统计估计的样本需求爆炸以及梯度传播的衰减。

Conclusion: 这些形式化的数学瓶颈在后来的AI突破中仅被缓解而未被彻底消除，表明形式化约束是理解AI发展历程的重要维度。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>
