Our results show a clear asymmetry: constraining attention layers with Stiefel geometry while assigning DGram geometry to MLP layers gives the best performance among the tested configurations, whereas the inverted assignment and all-DGram configuration become unstable under the shared hyperparameter setting. We trace this failure to singular value growth in DGram-constrained attention weights, which can amplify attention logits and induce softmax saturation. These findings suggest that symmetry-aware and geometry-aware optimization for transformers should be module-specific rather than uniform.

</details>


### [118] [Once-for-All: Scalable Simultaneous Forecasting via Equilibrium State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13285)
*Beinan Xu,Andy Song,Jiti Gao,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ESE是一种用于多系统同步预测的新范式，通过估计系统间的均衡状态并基于当前状态与均衡状态的差异生成整体预测，在保持与SOTA方法相当精度的同时实现10-70倍加速，具有线性时间复杂度、良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测方法每次只能预测一个系统，无法有效处理需要协调预测多个相互作用的系统（如经济学和医疗建模）的场景。

Method: 首先估计跨系统的均衡状态，然后基于当前状态与估计均衡状态之间的差异生成整体预测，采用线性时间复杂度算法。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据集（包括货币兑换和COVID-19传播建模）上，ESE至少与SOTA方法同样准确，同时速度显著更快，实现10-70倍加速，且随系统数量增加时扩展性显著优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: ESE是一种快速、可泛化、鲁棒且可扩展的多系统同步预测方法，能无缝集成传统预测器，兼具其精度与自身的高效率。

Abstract: We introduce Equilibrium State Estimation (ESE), a novel paradigm for simultaneous prediction, where multiple interacting systems require separate yet coordinated forecasts. Such scenarios often arise in real-world settings such as economics and healthcare modeling. Unlike existing approaches that predict one system at a time, ESE forecasts all systems in a single pass. It first estimates the equilibrium state across systems, then generates holistic forecasts based on the difference between the current state and the estimated equilibrium. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including currency exchange and COVID-19 spread modeling, demonstrate that ESE is at least as accurate as state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while being significantly faster. In addition, ESE integrates seamlessly with conventional predictors, combining their accuracy with its exceptional efficiency and delivering a 10-70x speedup. With linear-time complexity, ESE scales far better than SOTA methods as the number of systems increases. Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [119] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中梯度裁剪（gradient clipping）提供了理论依据，证明裁剪可以消除最大延迟对oracle复杂度的影响，并在次Weibull梯度噪声模型下给出了期望收敛和高概率收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步训练中慢节点导致的大延迟会负面影响收敛，而实践中梯度裁剪能"稳定"异步训练，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在为这一现象提供理论依据。

Method: 采用次Weibull分布对梯度噪声建模（推广了次高斯和次指数分布，涵盖深度学习中的重尾分布），理论分析梯度裁剪对ASGD收敛性的影响，证明了期望收敛和（异步优化中首次）高概率收敛。

Result: 证明了梯度裁剪能消除最大延迟在oracle复杂度中的依赖项；在次Weibull噪声模型下，实现了期望收敛保证以及异步优化中首次的高概率收敛保证。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪的理论有效性得到证明：它不仅在实践中稳定异步训练，理论上也能消除大延迟带来的负面收敛影响，为异步深度学习提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [120] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹量化敏感度分数(TQS)，通过动力系统稳定性视角重新定义训练后量化(PTQ)，实现了与量化过程解耦的先验敏感度估计，并构建了无需校准数据的混合精度框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法的敏感度分析与量化过程耦合，无法在量化器选择和位宽分配之前进行预算规划，且对黑盒或编译网络（含融合算子）缺乏适用性。

Method: 将网络的前向传播建模为离散时间动力系统，定义TQS度量量化误差在轨迹上的传播与放大效应，实现与量化器选择和位宽分配解耦的敏感度估计，并基于此构建混合精度量化框架。

Result: TQS-PTQ无需校准数据和二阶近似，在资源受限场景下实现了高性能的低精度部署。

Conclusion: 动力系统视角为低精度部署提供了一种稳健且高效的路径，尤其在资源受限场景中表现优异。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [121] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RGFiLM模块，通过稀有度门控的特征线性调制来解决上下文异常检测中因上下文分布不平衡导致的频繁误报问题，在海事轨迹异常检测任务中取得了最佳F1-FPR权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中上下文分布高度不平衡，稀有上下文区域是关键信息，但现有上下文条件模型在稀有上下文中会产生不稳定决策和过多误报。

Method: RGFiLM（Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation）：结合特征级调制（上下文条件缩放和平移隐藏特征）与数据驱动稀有度分数控制的门控机制，稀有上下文中门控更果断，频繁上下文中门控更保守。

Result: 在海事轨迹异常检测（使用AIS运动序列和ERA5环境上下文）中，RGFiLM在环境敏感绕行场景下取得了最佳平均F1-误报率权衡。

Conclusion: 显式考虑上下文稀有度是减少上下文敏感异常检测中误报的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [122] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出电力系统概率预测基准PowerPhase（支持最大36,964通道的AC潮流求解）与新型预测模型PowerForge（类型特定解码头+因果桥接），揭示安全-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测基准在通道规模（上限2,000）和电力系统约束评估上均存在不足，缺乏大规模、含物理约束的评估基准。

Method: 构建六套输电网（2,000-36,964通道）的PowerPhase基准，基于AC潮流生成轨迹，提出约束感知指标（Safety_mBrier, NECV, CVaR-alpha）。设计PowerForge模型，采用场景式分位数预测、类型特定解码头和变量组间因果桥接。

Result: 8个基线模型在3个随机种子上，分布准确性与约束满足产生不同排序（安全-保真度权衡）。PowerForge在所有电网上取得最优平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模电力系统概率预测需同时考虑分布精度和约束满足，PowerPhase为评估提供了系统性工具，PowerForge展示了有效平衡两者的设计思路。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [123] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需额外训练、仅在采样阶段进行的密度感知条件扩散模型方法，通过修改分类器梯度引导轨迹朝向低置信区域，并同时引导采样过程朝向预测的真实图像，从而在不损失生成质量的前提下提升对长尾/低密度区域的覆盖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导的扩散模型倾向于将概率质量集中在高密度类别均值附近，导致对类条件分布尾部稀有样本的覆盖不足。现有方法（如基于扩散的尾部采样）需要额外训练低密度搜索分类器和合成-真实判别器，代价高昂。

Method: 提出一种纯采样时间的密度感知引导方法：1) 利用预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器，修改逆动力学中的分类器梯度，将轨迹引导向低置信（低密度）区域；2) 同时引导采样过程朝向预测的真实图像。两项引导在噪声图像上应用，而非如多数方法在预测噪声上应用。

Result: 在64x64分辨率ADM模型上持续提升召回率（Recall），同时保持可比的FID；在256x256 ADM模型上展示了不同引导组合的视觉效果；标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导有助于生成高感知质量的样本。

Conclusion: 所提出的采样器无需额外训练即可提升长尾覆盖，且能与现有加速/蒸馏技术互补，为条件扩散模型生成多样性样本提供了高效的采样阶段解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [124] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM是一种无需3D真值、仅从单目视频的2D姿态学习3D人体运动先验的扩散模型框架，通过深度加权2D重投影损失近似3D监督，在HumanML3D上达到接近全3D监督的性能（FID 0.88 vs 0.54），并在真实视频数据集上生成人类偏好的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动生成模型依赖大规模3D动作捕捉数据，获取成本高昂且难以扩展。本文旨在仅利用易获取的单目视频2D姿态来学习3D运动先验，降低数据壁垒。

Method: (1) 使用预训练的2D-to-3D提升器提供近似3D序列作为噪声教师信号；(2) 对近似3D序列加噪后由模型去噪，再将预测结果重投影到2D空间与精确2D关键点对比监督；(3) 提出深度加权2D重投影损失，在期望上等价于直接3D监督；(4) 将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等3D正则化器适配到2D设置。

Result: HumanML3D上FID 0.88（全3D监督MDM为0.54），近乎缩小差距；在Fit3D和NBA真实视频数据集上生成的运动在人类偏好和定量指标上表现优异。

Conclusion: VideoMDM证明仅从2D姿态即可学习高质量的3D运动先验，训练阶段即构建连贯的3D运动流形，在合成和真实数据上均取得出色效果，为降低3D运动生成的数据依赖提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>


### [125] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了忆阻器模拟计算中位置编码导致ADC转换精度下降的问题，提出通过调整权重比例和ADC精度位可减少约50%的执行退化，或在无法修改ADC时通过去除编码相关线性变换减少约30%的退化，同时保持能耗稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器为自然语言处理中的神经网络模型提供了资源高效计算的新机遇，但当前这些器件在权重编程和执行过程中存在较大的信号失真问题，影响计算精度。

Method: 识别出变换后的位置编码的大输出值是导致忆阻器计算中模数转换（ADC）退化的主要原因；通过调整特定忆阻器层的权重比例和ADC精度位来降低退化；针对ADC不可修改的场景，通过去除编码相关的线性变换来缓解退化。

Result: 调整权重比例和ADC精度位可减少约50%的执行退化；在ADC不可修改时，去除编码相关线性变换可减少约30%的退化；两种方案均保持预估能耗稳定。

Conclusion: 忆阻器模拟计算中的位置编码输出值过大会显著影响ADC精度，通过合理调整硬件配置或修改编码方式可以有效缓解该问题，为忆阻器在NLP任务中的应用提供了优化方向。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [126] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有多模态变分自编码器在生成质量与跨模态一致性之间存在权衡。本文提出Hölder++，通过精确的Hölder池化、共享与私有表示分离以及层次化推理，有效改善了这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMVAE方法在生成多样性与跨模态语义一致性之间存在权衡，近期使用近似Hölder池化虽提升了一致性但牺牲了样本多样性。本文旨在改善这一生成质量-一致性的权衡。

Method: (i) 首次在多模态VAE中实现精确Hölder池化；(ii) 扩展架构建模独立的共享表示和模态特定私有表示；(iii) 引入层次化推理进一步增强共享与私有表示的分离。

Result: Hölder++持续改善了生成质量-一致性权衡，产生更有结构的潜在空间，且学习到的共享表示对下游任务具有信息量。

Conclusion: Hölder++通过精确Hölder池化、共享-私有表示分离与层次化推理，有效解决了多模态VAE中生成质量与一致性的权衡问题。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [127] [PolyFlow: Safe and Efficient Polytope-Constrained Flow Matching with Constraint Embedding and Projection-free Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13400)
*Jianming Ma,Qiyue Yang,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yazhou Zhang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyFlow提出了一种基于多面体约束的流匹配框架，通过将约束直接嵌入模型和流动力学中，实现了零约束违反、高分布保真度和低推理延迟，适用于安全关键物理系统中的规划与控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式生成模型在安全关键物理系统中部署时，由于严格的约束要求而面临挑战；传统的后验校正方法计算开销大且可能扭曲学习到的分布。

Method: PolyFlow提出了一种多面体约束的流匹配框架，包含离散时间流公式化和无投影架构，直接将约束嵌入到模型和流动力学中，无需昂贵的迭代求解器即可保证任意多面体约束的严格满足。

Result: PolyFlow在多个规划与控制任务中实现了零约束违反，同时保持较高的分布保真度；与最先进的约束生成基线相比，显著降低了推理延迟，并在安全性、效率和生成质量之间展现出有利的权衡。

Conclusion: PolyFlow通过将约束直接嵌入生成过程，有效解决了安全关键系统中流式生成模型的约束满足问题，在保证安全性的同时兼顾了生成质量和计算效率。

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on https://github.com/MJianM/PolyFlow.

</details>


### [128] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于扩散模型的投机性采样方案，能够高效实现原始投机采样机制，并首次将LLM中的块验证技术引入扩散模型，从而提升草案接受率。基于此，作者形式化了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式方法，相比现有投机方法可获得高达6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将投机解码从离散的LLM扩展到连续的扩散模型存在关键挑战：连续空间中残差分布的高效采样难以实现。现有方法要么计算效率低，要么依赖替代方案，无法直接应用原始投机采样机制。

Method: 提出一种新颖的采样方案，高效实现扩散模型的原始投机采样机制；将LLM中的块验证（block verification）适配到扩散模型，从理论上保证提升草案接受率；形式化并分析了无需训练的Free Drafter自投机草案生成方法。

Result: Free Drafter在无需额外训练、仅需现有并行验证开销的情况下，相比现有投机方法可获得最高6.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了离散投机解码与连续扩散模型投机采样之间的差距，通过块验证提升了采样效率，且Free Drafter作为无训练方案具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [129] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控动态调节记忆权重，在低分辨率下相较固定记忆权重的神经算子方法实现55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强神经算子使用固定记忆权重，无法适应不同观测条件（如分辨率、物理参数）下最优记忆权重的变化，限制了模型的适应性。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（Adaptive Memory Gated Fourier Neural Operator），引入可学习门控机制动态调节记忆权重，使模型能根据观测条件自适应调整记忆强度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Burgers方程上，低分辨率下实现55-79%的nRMSE降低；学习到的门控值随分辨率提高从约0.7自动降至接近零。

Conclusion: 自适应记忆门控机制有效提升了神经算子在不同观测条件下的泛化能力，验证了动态调节记忆权重的必要性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [130] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络(GNN)的自学习框架，用于无监督图聚类。该框架通过多轮迭代，每轮使用GNN生成节点表示并聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮用于生成表示的图结构，同时利用每轮构建的上下文图来增强表示。实验表明该方法能有效融合网络边和节点属性信息，在合成数据上优于仅关注网络或属性的方法，多轮学习优于单轮训练，在真实数据上对平衡聚类具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类方法大多仅利用网络边信息，而真实场景中节点属性也包含重要信息。现有方法虽尝试同时利用两者，但在无监督设置下有效融合图结构和节点属性仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种多轮自学习框架：每轮中GNN生成节点表示用于聚类，该聚类结果用于重新构建下一轮所需的图结构（影响节点表示生成），同时每轮利用原始图构建上下文图辅助表示学习，整个流程在完全无监督下进行。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法能从边和属性中提取信息，当两者信息量都不高时优于仅关注单一模态的方法；多轮学习始终优于单轮长训练；在真实平衡聚类数据集上达到与最先进方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自学习迭代机制有效融合了图结构和节点属性，在无监督图聚类任务上表现出色，尤其适用于聚类大小平衡的场景。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [131] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于预训练分子扩散模型的逐样本不确定性估计后处理方法，基于拉普拉斯近似测量去噪轨迹中的噪声预测变异性，该分数与样本质量负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中被广泛使用，但缺乏判断生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，因此需要一种能够评估生成样本不确定性的方法。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，在生成轨迹上测量噪声预测的变异性，从而得到逐样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本质量指标呈负相关；该分数可用于过滤生成样本，通过测试时缩放提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验不确定性估计方法能有效指示分子生成质量，并可作为测试时质量控制的工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [132] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将神经网络编辑形式化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，通过两种环境（MaskWorld和ShiftWorld）让代理从奖励反馈中学习编辑策略，在文本分类的偏见消除和图像分类的机器遗忘任务上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络编辑需要针对特定目标设计专门的算法，过程耗时且需要大量人工努力。作者希望提出一种通用框架，让编辑策略可以自动学习而非手动设计。

Method: 将神经网络编辑建模为强化学习问题，设计了两个环境：MaskWorld（对权重进行乘性缩放）和ShiftWorld（对权重进行加性更新）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标，代理通过奖励反馈学习编辑策略。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，学习到的策略将遗忘集准确率降至接近0%，同时保留集准确率保持在90%以上。在偏见消除任务中，学习到的策略将偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持一般分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经网络编辑可以被形式化为强化学习问题，使得编辑策略能够从奖励反馈中学习，而无需为每个任务手动设计专门算法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [133] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof 是一个面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过将 M3 模型的证明生成、验证和修复能力整合，在测试时进行种群搜索和锦标赛选择，使 M3 在 IMO 2025 和 USAMO 2026 上分别达到 35/42 和 36/42，超越人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决竞赛级数学证明问题中，单个模型生成证明质量有限的问题，通过测试时扩展（test-time scaling）利用群体搜索提升证明的正确性和可靠性。

Method: 首先训练 M3 模型具备三种面向证明的能力：证明生成、证明验证、基于批评条件的证明修复，其中验证器采用深度防御生成式验证器以保持低假阳性率。测试时，MaxProof 将模型同时用作生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，在候选证明种群中进行搜索，并通过锦标赛选择输出最终证明。

Result: 在 IMO 2025 上获得 35/42 分，在 USAMO 2026 上获得 36/42 分，均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof 的测试时扩展策略能有效提升模型在竞赛数学证明上的表现，达到超越人类金牌水平的成绩。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [134] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个超分子化学基准SupraBench，用于系统评估LLM在超分子主客体推理（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述及分子识别）上的能力，并发布16M token语料SupraPMC。实验表明LLM在所有任务上仍有显著提升空间，领域适应预训练在分布内回归任务有效但会牺牲严格格式输出，不同任务难度差异揭示了当前LLM在超分子化学推理中的具体不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分子主客体系统设计依赖耗时的湿实验验证，而LLM虽在分子结合任务中表现优异，但缺乏系统评估其在超分子化学推理能力的基准。

Method: 与领域专家合作构建SupraBench，包含四项基础任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一项辅助视觉任务；同时从Europe PMC蒸馏出16M token的超分子化学语料SupraPMC用于领域适应预训练，并评测多种开源与闭源LLM。

Result: LLM在所有任务上仍存在显著性能差距；领域适应预训练对分布内回归任务有效，但会牺牲严格格式输出；不同任务难度差异揭示了LLM在超分子化学推理中的不同失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在超分子化学推理上仍有较大提升空间，SupraBench可作为评估LLM在化学领域推理能力的重要基准，领域适应预训练需权衡任务性能与输出格式的严格性。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [135] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一个全无监督的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过结合四种小波特征族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）和五个结构化孤立森林，能够检测点异常、分布异常、时间异常和集体异常四种类型，在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上平均F1=0.228，VUS-PR达0.463，超越此前最优方法40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列中存在四种结构迥异的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常、集体异常），每种类型需要不同的特征表示，而现有无监督方法大多只针对其中一两种类型，且可解释性有限。

Method: 生成K=500个跨四种小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机分析小波特征，每种小波族针对特定异常类型；将特征输入五个结构化孤立森林（每种类型一个+一个元IF处理复合异常）；使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测；通过分支触发机制直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在mTSBench全部19个数据集上平均F1=0.228（13个可检测数据集F1=0.322），VUS-PR=0.463，超越此前最优0.329达40.7%；消融实验验证自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均至关重要；诊断框架识别出6个数据集本质上无法被任何无监督方法检测。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF是首个统一处理四种异常类型的全无监督框架，无需数据集特定调参即达到SOTA性能，并通过构造性设计实现内建可解释性，同时提出了诊断框架来界定无监督异常检测的理论极限。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [136] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，在理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布，实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在序列生成中表现优异，但针对任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调方法尚未被充分探索，现有方法局限于固定长度设置。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本的理论收敛保证；建立去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可行方法；提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，可证明获得生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性方面均优于现有的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一的理论框架，兼具理论保证和实证有效性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [137] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整的杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），该层通过硬编码杯积并引入高规范理论中的调整项，实现了设计上的规范不变性。主要理论结果表明，在闭合环路上，输出完全依赖于调整系数；将该系数设为零则输出完全消失，表明调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源。该可观测量是一个非零的二次型，且在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学和几何学中的许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积。作者希望设计一种神经层，能够硬编码这种杯积结构并引入调整项，从而实现规范不变性，使模型能够直接学习并输出物理上合理的规范不变可观测量。

Method: 引入了调整的杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），这是一种神经网络基本单元，硬编码了杯积运算，并从高规范理论中引入了一个调整项，使得读出结果在设计上即满足规范不变性。

Result: 理论分析表明，在闭合环路上，输出完全由调整系数决定；若将该系数设为零，则无论其他参数如何，输出完全消失。因此，调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源。该可观测量被证明是一个非零的二次型，且在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整的杯积神经层能够生成规范不变的输出，且其信号完全源自调整项。这一设计为在神经网络中嵌入物理对称性提供了新思路，可应用于需要规范不变性的物理和几何问题中。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [138] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重构为未来可观测性推断和数值估计的联合问题，通过双流架构（观测流与数值流）及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，在包含自然缺失和工业数据的Shadow基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如神经ODE、连续时间图网络）虽改进了历史不规则性的建模，但推理时仍隐式假设未来有效观测的时间戳已知，这在许多实际系统中不成立——更根本的问题不仅是未来值是多少，还包括是否会发生有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架，包含：(1) 观测流和数值流双流架构；(2) 三个耦合模块：可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导的依赖建模、联合预测；(3) 构建包含自然缺失和工业数据的Shadow基准；(4) 提出观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）指标综合评估耦合可预测性。

Result: 在Shadow基准上的大量实验表明，Timeflies一致优于现有方法，验证了显式建模未来可观测性对含缺失值时间序列预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式建模未来观测的可观测性对于时间序列预测至关重要，Timeflies通过联合推断可观测性和数值估计实现了更贴近实际需求的预测框架。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [139] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明当学习器能访问数据生成过程的模拟器时，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复经典独立数据下的VC维泛化保证，并展示条件采样的统计与计算优势。还提出一个能同时学习所有多项式时间可采样过程的单一算法，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制，显著拓展了经典PAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中的泛化结果大多依赖于数据独立性假设，对强依赖数据的学习问题研究有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究在数据生成过程高度复杂且依赖的情况下，如何仍能获得可靠的学习保证。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"框架，假设学习器可以访问一个近似数据生成分布的模拟器；在此框架下分析学习保证，并利用条件采样展示统计与计算优势；最后提出一个统一算法，同时学习所有在多项式时间内可采样的过程。

Result: 在可模拟过程框架下，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复与经典独立数据设定相同的VC维相关误差界；证明了条件采样在此设定下的严格优势；提出的单一算法能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程，遗憾由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作从概念上拓宽了经典PAC学习模型，表明在具备模拟器访问能力的情况下，强依赖数据的泛化问题可以得到与独立数据情形同样有效的处理。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [140] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCSB提出了一种基于概率单纯形约束的稀疏装袋框架，通过凹二次惩罚解决L1单纯形悖论，实现集成剪枝与概率校准的联合优化，最高可压缩96%的集成规模并提升校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准装袋集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM等）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了各基估计器在不同局部区域的能力差异，且导致模型过度自信（overconfidence）。

Method: 1) 将集成剪枝与校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，最小化袋外（OOB）损失；2) 针对L1范数在单纯形上为常数、无法实现稀疏化的"L1单纯形悖论"，引入凹二次惩罚项诱导稀疏性。

Result: 最高实现96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升，预期校准误差（ECE）显著降低，泛化精度保持或提升。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种模型无关的后训练压缩与校准框架，能够在不牺牲精度的情况下大幅减小集成规模并改善概率输出质量。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现链式思维推理中存在一个"承诺边界"——从临时猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变，该转变通常在一个步骤内完成，后续步骤对最终答案无影响。基于此，通过早退机制可平均减少55%的推理长度而不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理中每个步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确，理解答案形成过程有助于优化推理效率。

Method: 通过早退估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性；使用注意力探针从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；在承诺边界处早退推理块以缩短推理链。

Result: 推理通常在一个单步内完成从临时猜测到稳定答案的承诺边界转变；后续步骤是附带的（epiphenomenal），不改变最终答案概率；注意力探针可高精度解码答案形成阶段并泛化到未见任务；早退可平均减少55%的CoT长度且性能损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 链式思维推理中存在承诺边界这一结构特征，利用该特征可大幅提升推理效率，表明部分推理步骤是冗余的。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [142] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过恢复子空间维度（k_t）等几何度量，发现连续学习中的灾难性遗忘并非信息破坏，而是可及性和流形对齐问题——遗忘知识仍可通过低维子空间（平均k_t=8.0）紧凑解码，主角度漂移与可恢复性高度相关（r=-0.862），支持稳定恢复流形假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"灾难性遗忘是知识破坏"的传统观点，基于可及性坍塌框架，从几何结构角度探究连续学习中的可恢复性问题。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，提出恢复子空间维度（k_t）度量（保持90%全探测性能所需的最小奇异方向数），分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度。

Result: 恢复维度在训练中保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移强预测可恢复性（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说：遗忘知识在表征重组后仍可紧凑解码；灾难性遗忘主要是可及性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [143] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性（坐标稀疏、集中在FFN层）和几何上低秩但谱集中的特点，且密集教师监督并未导致普通密集参数重写，而是保留了在线策略训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了在线策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督两种策略，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不明确，作者希望从参数更新的稀疏性和几何特性角度理解这一混合训练方式如何改变模型。

Method: 在多个语言与视觉-语言模型对上，通过分析OPD更新的稀疏性（分布特性、子网络训练有效性、优化器对比）和几何特性（秩、谱分布、与源权重的奇异子空间关系）进行实证分析。

Result: 1) OPD更新具有坐标稀疏性，分布在各层且主要集中在FFN层；仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复接近完整OPD的性能。2) SGD优化器效果不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异质坐标梯度尺度。3) 更新数值上满秩但谱集中，主要偏离源权重的奇异子空间，且落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将OPD转变为普通密集参数重写；OPD保留了在线策略训练的重要几何特征，表明其本质仍是一种在线策略后训练方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [144] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了图神经网络中位置编码（PEs）的截断版本，发现截断后的谱编码和游走编码在表达能力上存在根本差异，且截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，实验表明混合使用截断PEs优于单一类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用截断版位置编码（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂），但这些截断版本的理论性质尚不清楚，论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论分析截断后不同家族PEs的表达能力差异，研究k-调和距离等谱PEs族，并结合实验验证。

Result: 截断后谱PEs和游走PEs的表达能力存在根本差异；截断谱PEs不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断PEs（如k-调和距离）间也有显著差异；混合截断PEs在真实数据集上优于单一类型。

Conclusion: 截断处理会导致不同PEs家族的表达能力发生本质分化，实践时应考虑混合使用多种截断PEs以达到更优效果。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Effective Geometry and Position-Dependent Mass in Dual-$q$ Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12444)
*A. Boumali,A. Makhlouf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Borges提出的变形导数形式，重点分析线性算子D_(q)与其非线性对偶算子D^(q)之间的关系。通过坐标和波函数的联合变换，将非线性问题转化为变形坐标中的线性薛定谔方程，等效于厄密的位置依赖质量哈密顿量。变形参数q决定有效质量分布和度量。应用于自由粒子、无限深方势阱、方势垒和弱变形谐振子，发现q<1时约束长度减小，能谱升高、隧穿增强；q>1时约束长度增加，能谱降低、隧穿抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Borges变形导数形式中将对偶导数直接代入动能项导致的非线性薛定谔方程问题，恢复叠加原理和概率解释的常规意义。

Method: 通过坐标和波函数的联合变换，将非线性问题映射为变形坐标中的线性薛定谔方程，等价于厄密的位置依赖质量哈密顿量。

Result: 自由粒子、无限深方势阱、方势垒和弱变形谐振子的解；q<1时约束长度减小、能谱升高、隧穿增强；q>1时约束长度增加、能谱降低、隧穿抑制。

Conclusion: Borges对偶q框架提供了与Costa Filho等人非加性平移方法不同的另一种路径，但导向相同的有效几何结构。

Abstract: This work investigates the deformed-derivative formalism introduced by Borges, with emphasis on the relation between the linear operator $D_{(q)}$ and its nonlinear dual counterpart $D^{(q)}$. Directly inserting the dual derivative into the kinetic term leads to a nonlinear Schrödinger equation and obscures the usual interpretation of superposition and probability. We show that this nonlinearity can be removed by a simultaneous transformation of the coordinate and of the wave function. The transformed problem is an ordinary linear Schrödinger equation in a deformed coordinate, and its representation in the physical coordinate is equivalent to a Hermitian position-dependent-mass (PDM) Hamiltonian. In this formulation, the deformation parameter $q$ determines both the effective mass profile and the associated metric. The formalism is applied to the free particle, the infinite square well, the rectangular barrier, and the harmonic oscillator in the weak-deformation regime. Comparison with the nonadditive-translation approach of Costa Filho \emph{et al.} shows that the Borges dual-$q$ framework provides an alternative route to the same effective geometric structure. For $q<1$, the effective confinement length is reduced, which raises the bound-state spectrum and enhances tunneling; for $q>1$, the effective length is increased, which lowers the spectrum and suppresses tunneling relative to the undeformed limit $q=1$.

</details>


### [146] [SAT, MaxSAT, and SMT for QLDPC Distance Computation: A Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12445)
*Yu-Fang Chen,Seyed Mohammad Reza Jafari,Ching-Yi Lai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于SAT和MaxSAT的精确量子LDPC码距离计算方法，发现：XOR感知推理并无系统优势，Brouwer-Zimmermann搜索在QLDPC场景下失去传统优势，分支定界MaxSAT显著优于不满足核MaxSAT，表明求解器架构和优化策略对可扩展性起决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的精确距离计算对验证候选容错量子码构造至关重要，但该问题的计算结构尚未被充分理解，不清楚哪些算法原则支配着实际可扩展性以及哪类精确求解器最适合此任务。

Method: 对代表性量子LDPC码进行基于SAT和MaxSAT的精确距离计算系统研究，并与多种已有精确距离方法进行比较分析。

Result: (1) 实际可扩展性更多由基数约束和优化边界的处理决定，而非奇偶推理；(2) Brouwer-Zimmermann搜索在QLDPC场景下失去传统可扩展性优势；(3) 分支定界MaxSAT显著优于不满足核MaxSAT。

Conclusion: 挑战并修正了关于精确QLDPC距离计算的若干主流直觉，揭示了求解器架构和优化策略对实际可扩展性的决定性作用。

Abstract: Exact distance computation for quantum LDPC (QLDPC) codes plays a central role in validating candidate fault-tolerant quantum-code constructions, yet the computational structure of this problem remains poorly understood. Despite substantial recent progress in QLDPC design, it remains unclear which algorithmic principles govern the practical scalability of exact distance computation and which classes of exact solvers are best suited to this task. To address these questions, we conduct a systematic study of SAT- and MaxSAT-based formulations for exact QLDPC distance computation across representative codes. We further compare these formulations against several established exact-distance approaches in order to better understand the algorithmic landscape of exact QLDPC distance computation. Our study challenges and refines several prevailing intuitions about exact QLDPC distance computation. First, despite the XOR-rich structure of QLDPC parity checks, practical scalability appears to be governed more by the handling of cardinality constraints and optimization bounds than by parity reasoning alone. Accordingly, XOR-aware reasoning does not provide a systematic advantage across our benchmark suite. Second, Brouwer-Zimmermann-style search, long regarded as the benchmark paradigm for exact distance computation in sparse classical codes, no longer maintains its traditional scalability advantage in the QLDPC setting. This finding challenges the expectation that techniques successful for sparse classical codes remain dominant for QLDPC codes. Third, substantial qualitative differences arise even among MaxSAT solvers themselves. Branch-and-bound MaxSAT significantly outperforms unsat-core-based MaxSAT on challenging benchmarks, demonstrating that solver architecture and optimization strategy play a decisive role in practical scalability.

</details>


### [147] [Theoretical Study for Generating Optical GKP State via a Single-Photon-Added Squeezed Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12467)
*Deriyan Senjaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用单光子增加压缩真空态（SPSV）注入50:50分束器，通过其中一输出端条件测量产生光学GKP态的理论方案。在3.76 dB压缩水平下，该方案保真度最高可达85%，优于基于奇薛定谔猫态的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找更简单、实验上更易实现的光学GKP态制备途径，避免使用薛定谔猫态等复杂资源。

Method: 将单光子增加压缩真空态（由压缩参数r定义）注入50:50分束器，通过在一个输出端口进行条件测量，将量子态投影到近似有限能量GKP态的形式上。

Result: 在3.76 dB压缩水平下，方案达到最高85%的保真度，性能优于基于压缩光奇薛定谔猫态的方法。

Conclusion: 单光子增加压缩真空态是制备光学GKP态的一种实用且有效的途径，为容错光量子计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A theoretical framework is developed to analyze the generation of the optical GKP state using a single-photon-added squeezed vacuum. This state, defined by the squeezing parameter $r$, is injected into a 50:50 beam splitter, and the optical GKP state is obtained through conditional measurement at one output port. The single-photon-added squeezed vacuum is especially prominent in this context because it provides a simpler and more experimentally accessible ingredient than Schrodinger cat states, while conditional measurement ensures projection onto a state that closely approximates the finite-energy GKP form. Fidelity is employed to quantify this closeness, and the analysis demonstrates that the scheme achieves a maximum fidelity of 85% at a squeezing level of $3.76 \ \text{dB}$. This performance surpasses approaches based on squeezed optical odd Schrodinger cat states, underscoring the single-photon-added squeezed vacuum as a practical and effective pathway toward fault-tolerant photonic quantum computing.

</details>


### [148] [Geometric Algebra Quantum Gate Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12480)
*Youssef Amraoui,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在复几何代数框架下重新表述了Pauli群和Clifford群，揭示了Pauli算子的几何本质是定向子空间（叶片），证明了Clifford算子可由π/4-Pauli转子生成，并提出了贪心Pauli转子分解算法，最后给出了Clifford+T通用性的π/8转子几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩阵和张量积形式化描述量子门往往掩盖了其几何结构，作者希望利用复几何代数框架为Pauli群和Clifford群提供更自然的几何解释。

Method: 在复几何代数框架中，将Pauli群自然识别为全局相位下的叶片群（blade group），证明Clifford算子由π/4-Pauli转子的乘积生成，并提出贪心Pauli转子分解算法，最后将Clifford+T通用性解释为π/8转子。

Result: 证明了Pauli群与叶片群的自然对应关系，给出了Clifford算子的几何生成机制，贪心算法经验上显示出Clifford算子具有出人意料的紧凑分解，以及Clifford+T的几何解释。

Conclusion: 几何代数框架为量子门特别是Pauli和Clifford群提供了更直观的几何理解，有望推动量子计算中几何代数方法的发展。

Abstract: Quantum gates are usually described through matrix and tensor-product formalisms that often obscure their geometric structure. In this work, we formulate the Pauli and Clifford groups within the complex Geometric Algebra (GA) framework. We show that the Pauli group is naturally identified with the group of blades up to a global phase, thereby providing a geometric interpretation of Pauli operators and their commutation relations in terms of oriented subspaces. We further prove that Clifford operators are generated by products of π/4-Pauli rotors and introduce a greedy Pauli rotor decomposition algorithm whose empirical behavior suggests unexpectedly compact decompositions for Clifford operators. Finally, we show that Clifford+T universality admits a natural geometric interpretation through π/8-rotors within this framework.

</details>


### [149] [Statistical Mechanics and Symmetries of Non-Abelian Anyon Proliferation: From Deformation to Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12527)
*Avi Vadali,Robijn Vanhove,Ruben Verresen,Jason Alicea,Pablo Sala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿贝尔拓扑序在波函数形变和退相干下的稳定性，通过统计力学模型和蒙特卡洛模拟发现，$D_4$拓扑序在非阿贝尔任意子增殖超过阈值时会因寄生凝聚共享的阿贝尔融合结果而崩溃，且得到的平庸相会"记住"哪种任意子发生了凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子计算依赖编织非阿贝尔任意子，但需要拓扑序在态制备不完美和环境噪声下保持稳定，因此需要理解拓扑序在这些条件下的稳定性机制。

Method: 建立统计力学模型框架，其对称性自然揭示破坏性的任意子激发；结合蒙特卡洛模拟研究$D_4$拓扑序在波函数形变和量子信道（增殖多种非阿贝尔任意子）下的稳定性。

Result: 发现超过有限阈值后，两种非阿贝尔任意子的增殖会寄生凝聚其共享的阿贝尔任意子融合结果，从而破坏拓扑序；所得的平庸相与凝聚所有阿贝尔电荷得到的平庸相不同，即该相"记住"了哪些任意子发生了凝聚。

Conclusion: 该对称性框架能清晰区分不同的平庸相，为基于综合征测量的非阿贝尔拓扑序最优解码器识别相关对称性提供了第一步基础。

Abstract: Topological quantum computation relies on braiding non-Abelian anyons, but requires the underlying topological order to survive imperfect state preparation and environmental noise. We show that the instability of topological order to wavefunction deformations and to decoherence, with the latter probed by syndrome distributions, are generically captured by stat-mech models whose symmetries naturally expose the corrupting anyonic excitations. As an example, we combine this framework with Monte-Carlo simulations to resolve the stability of $D_4$ topological order under deformations and quantum channels that proliferate multiple non-Abelian anyon species that individually are unable to condense. We show that beyond a finite threshold, proliferation of two non-Abelian anyon species parasitically condenses a shared Abelian-anyon fusion outcome, destroying the topological order. Our symmetry-based approach sharply differentiates the resulting trivial phase from that obtained by condensing all Abelian charges; in other words, the trivial phase "remembers" which anyons condensed. This framework provides a first step into identifying the relevant symmetry for optimal decoders, conditioned on syndrome measurements, of non-Abelian topological order.

</details>


### [150] [Unifying spacetime approaches to quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12539)
*N. L. Diaz,M. Cerezo,Paolo Braccia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出“时空态”作为时空量子力学框架的核心对象，统一了路径积分、量子态随时间的演化、伪密度矩阵、Page-Wootters机制、超密度算符和类时纠缠等多种现有形式体系，并展示了其在Leggett-Garg不等式、OTOC、时间张量网络、费米子系统、相对论性QFT、量子参考系和经典物理等广泛领域的应用与洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子力学公式化中出现了大量时空导向的方法，但这些方法之间的关系不明确，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来解释它们之间的内在联系以及各自捕获的时空信息。

Method: 通过定义“时空态”作为基本对象，利用特定求值、信息约化、线性映射或量子通道等手段，将路径积分、量子态随时间的演化、伪密度矩阵、Page-Wootters机制、超密度算符、类时纠缠等不同形式体系纳入统一描述，并提供显式的数学表示来揭示它们之间的等价与关联关系。

Result: 证明了多种时空量子力学方法本质上是同一底层对象的不同表现形式，建立了它们之间的显式数学联系，并澄清了各自所捕获的时空信息内容。

Conclusion: 时空态框架提供了一个统一的视角来理解量子力学中时空信息处理的各种方法，并对Leggett-Garg不等式、OTOC、时间张量网络、费米子系统、相对论性QFT、量子参考系以及经典物理等领域提供了新的见解和指导。

Abstract: Recent efforts to formulate quantum mechanics in a way that treats space and time on a more equal footing have led to a large variety of spacetime-oriented approaches. In this work we present a detailed study of spacetime states, the objects that play the role of quantum states in the recently introduced framework of spacetime quantum mechanics, and show that the main proposals in the literature are different manifestations of the same underlying object. Path integrals, quantum states over time, pseudo-density matrices, the Page and Wootters mechanism, superdensity operators, and timelike-entanglement proposals all arise from spacetime states through particular evaluations, reduced information, linear maps, or quantum channels. This unification provides explicit mathematical representations of these formalisms, reveals relations among them, and clarifies the spacetime information each one captures. We also study the broader relevance of the spacetime-state point of view for Leggett-Garg inequalities, OTOCs, temporal tensor networks, fermionic systems, relativistic QFTs, quantum reference frames, and classical physics, together with additional insights and perspectives revealed by the common unifying framework.

</details>


### [151] [Spectral analysis of equilibration: information leakage in isolated quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12545)
*André T. Cesário,Marcos G. Alpino,Reinaldo O. Vianna,Tiago Debarba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于子空间粗粒化的统一动力学-谱框架来研究孤立量子系统的平衡化，核心是泄漏保真度函数（LFF），它直接量化信息流和记忆损失，无需系综假设或微扰论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孤立量子系统平衡化理论缺乏一个统一、状态依赖且无需系综假设或微扰方法的几何直观框架来刻画信息泄漏与不可逆性之间的关系。

Method: 基于子空间粗粒化方法，定义泄漏保真度函数（LFF）作为核心量，结合谱间隙结构、有效维度、谱功率分布、熵度量以及量子速度极限，推导出通用界限并建立动力学-谱联系。

Result: 推导了LFF时间涨落的通用界限，表明大谱离域抑制涨落并保证平均平衡化；建立了相位混合、间隙参与度与动力学稳定性之间的定量联系；通过量子速度极限揭示了平衡化时间尺度。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了状态依赖、几何透明的视角，阐明谱复杂性和子空间信息泄漏如何共同支配封闭量子多体系统的不可逆性。

Abstract: We develop a unified dynamical-spectral framework for equilibration in isolated quantum systems based on a subspace coarse-graining approach. Central to our formulation is the Leakage Fidelity Function (LFF), defined as the probability that a unitarily evolving state escapes the support of its initial subspace. This quantity provides a direct, operational measure of information flow and memory loss without invoking ensemble assumptions or perturbative arguments. We derive universal bounds on temporal fluctuations of the LFF, in terms of the spectral gap structure and the square of the effective dimension, evincing that large spectral delocalization suppresses fluctuations and guarantees equilibration on average. By introducing spectral power distributions and associated entropic measures, we establish a quantitative link between phase mixing, gap participation, and dynamical stability. We further investigate the equilibration timescale by connecting the LFF to quantum speed limits, thereby revealing the average time required for equilibration. Our results provide a state-dependent, geometrically transparent perspective on how spectral complexity and subspace information leakage jointly govern irreversibility in closed quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [152] [New bounds on private simultaneous quantum message passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12557)
*Uma Girish,Alex May,Natalie Parham,Henry Yuen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了私有同步消息（PSM）模型在量子设置中的上下界。下界方面：1) Nečiporuk度量限制了完美正确性下k方量子PSM所需的纠缠量，对显式函数给出二次下界；2) 通信矩阵的秩限制了有完美隐私但不完美正确性的两方量子PSM，这蕴含了经典PSM的新下界。上界方面：1) 给出了基于量子电路大小s、深度d_f等参数的PSM复杂度上界；2) 傅里叶1范数的平方限制了经典PSM复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSM模型作为安全多方计算的最小模型，其通信和相关成本的理解仍然不足，特别是在量子设置中。本文旨在填补量子PSM模型上下界的研究空白。

Method: 下界：使用Nečiporuk度量和通信矩阵秩的方法。上界：推广基于T-深度的NLQC技术从2方到k方，并考虑Clifford层光锥受限的情况；利用傅里叶分析。

Result: 给出了量子PSM的二次下界和经典PSM的新下界；给出了基于量子电路参数的PSM上界，以及基于傅里叶1范数的经典PSM上界。

Conclusion: 首次在量子PSM中利用隐私条件给出下界，并将NLQC技术从2方推广到多方，扩展了PSM与量子计算复杂度的联系。

Abstract: In the private simultaneous message (PSM) setting, $k$ players obtain inputs $x_i\in\{0,1\}^n$ and then each send messages to a referee, who should learn $f(x_1,...,x_k)$ but no other information about $(x_1,...,x_k)$. The PSM setting was introduced as a minimal model for secure multiparty computation and has connections to Boolean function complexity. In the quantum setting, PSM has been related to non-local quantum computation (NLQC). The communication and correlation cost of implementing PSM remains poorly understood. Here, we give new upper and lower bounds on the (quantum) PSM model. For lower bounds, we show: 1) Nečiporuk's measure lower bounds the entanglement required for $k$-player quantum PSM with perfect correctness. This leads to quadratic lower bounds for explicit functions. 2) The rank of the communication matrix of $f(x_1,x_2)$ lower bounds 2-player quantum PSM with perfect privacy but imperfect correctness. This implies a previously unknown lower bound on classical PSM with imperfect correctness. When allowing quantum communication and shared entanglement, these are the first lower bounds on quantum PSM that make use of the privacy condition. For upper bounds, we show: 1) Letting $s$ be the size of a quantum circuit computing $f$, $d_f$ be the circuit depth, $k$ the number of players, $n$ the number of bits received by each player, and $ε$ a correctness parameter, we obtain $\mathsf{PSM}_k^*(f) \leq (kn +s) \cdot \log^{O(d_f)}(s/ε)$. 2) The square of the Fourier 1 norm of $f$, $\Vert \hat{f}\Vert_1^2$, upper bounds the classical PSM complexity, $\mathsf{PSM}(f)\leq O(\Vert \hat{f} \Vert^2_1)$. In proving the first upper bound, we generalize existing $T$-depth based techniques for NLQC from $2$ to $k\geq 2$ parties, and consider cases where the Clifford layers are restricted to having small light cones.

</details>


### [153] [Roto-Reflection Geometry of Pure Two-Qubit Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12637)
*Stanislav Filatov,Marcis Auzinsh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了纯两比特纠缠的几何本质：最大纠缠态对应两个局域Bloch球面之间的 improper正交映射（roto-reflection），部分纠缠态通过分离concurrence的收缩后恢复相同的几何结构，并定义了纠缠旋转反射平面（ERRP）作为纠缠标量幅度的协变几何补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上纯两比特纠缠仅用concurrence等标量描述，缺乏几何直观。作者试图揭示纠缠的天然几何形式，为纠缠提供协变的几何框架。

Method: 在Pauli关联张量中分析两比特态，识别最大纠缠态对应两个Bloch球面间的improper正交映射（roto-reflection）。对部分纠缠纯态，通过分离与concurrence相关的收缩，恢复相同的roto-reflection几何，定义ERRP。

Result: 最大纠缠态表现为roto-reflection映射；部分纠缠态在分离收缩后同样呈现该几何结构；ERRP组织了两比特态的最大关联方向。

Conclusion: 纠缠不仅具有标量幅度（concurrence），还拥有自然的协变几何形式（ERRP），二者互为补充，提供了更完整的纠缠刻画。

Abstract: Pure two-qubit entanglement is usually characterized by scalar quantities such as concurrence. Here we show that it also has a natural geometric form. In the Pauli correlation tensor, maximally entangled states appear as improper orthogonal maps between two local Bloch spheres. These maps are roto-reflections. For partially entangled pure states, the same roto-reflection geometry is recovered after separating the contraction associated with concurrence. We call the corresponding geometric object the Entanglement Roto-Reflection Plane (ERRP). It organizes the maximally correlated directions of the two-qubit state and provides a covariant geometric complement to the scalar magnitude of entanglement.

</details>


### [154] [Supersymmetry of dissipative Bose-Fermi systems with application to Jaynes-Cummings and Dicke models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12682)
*Colin V. Coane,Francesco Iachello*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用超对称性将Jaynes-Cummings模型和Dicke模型（在旋转波近似下）的Hamiltonians及相应马尔可夫开放系统的Liouvillians写成矩阵形式，并给出显式解析本征值解。这些结果是基于系统与超代数u(1|1)及其推广的交换关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为耦合玻色-费米系统（如Jaynes-Cummings和Dicke模型）的Hamiltonians和Liouvillians提供解析本征值求解方法，利用超对称结构简化计算。

Method: 利用Hamiltonian与超代数u(1|1)的线性Casimir不变量对易的性质，以及Dicke模型额外与su(2)代数对易的性质，将Hamiltonians和Liouvillians表示为矩阵形式，进而求解本征值。

Result: 成功将Jaynes-Cummings和Dicke模型在旋转波近似下的Hamiltonians以及相应马尔可夫开放系统的Liouvillians置于矩阵形式，并获得了显式解析本征值解。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于具有u(1|1)乃至更一般u(n|m)动力学超代数的各类耦合玻色-费米系统，为更复杂系统的研究提供了高效工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate how supersymmetries of Hamiltonians for coupled Bose-Fermi systems can be used to place the Hamiltonians of the Jaynes-Cummings model and Dicke model under the rotating wave approximation in matrix form and provide explicit analytic solutions for their eigenvalues. We then use this supersymmetry to place the Liouvillians of the associated Markovian open systems in matrix form and provide explicit solutions for their eigenvalues. These results are a consequence of the fact that the Hamiltonian of the Jaynes-Cummings model commutes with the linear Casimir invariant of the superalgebra $u(1|1)$ and that the Hamiltonian of the Dicke model commutes both with the linear invariant of $\sum_{i} u_{i}(1|1)$ and with the invariant of an additional $su(2)$ algebra. Our methods apply to various coupled Bose-Fermi systems with $u(1|1)$ and more generally with $u(n|m)$ dynamical superalgebras, and may provide efficient tools for studying more complicated examples.

</details>


### [155] [Multiple Topological Haldane Phases for Symmetry-Protected Quantum Information Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12685)
*João Pedro Gama D'Elia,Irene D'Amico,Thereza Paiva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将自旋1/2梯子系统分割成多个独立的Haldane相子系统，利用对称保护拓扑序作为可扩展的量子计算架构，通过有限尺寸效应实现单量子比特旋转，通过伊辛型相互作用实现纠缠门，从而用两个易实验调控的参数驱动通用量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称保护拓扑相具有受保护的边缘态和抗扰动能力，在量子信息处理方面有潜在应用，但如何将其用于实用高效的量子计算仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 将单个自旋1/2梯子系统分割成多个独立的Haldane相子系统，在每个子系统的$S^z=0$扇区的两个拓扑态中编码量子比特；利用有限尺寸效应产生的可控能级分裂，通过局域磁场实现单量子比特旋转；利用相邻子系统边缘间的伊辛型相互作用实现纠缠门。

Result: 仅需两个易实验调控的控制参数即可驱动通用量子计算，证明了对称保护拓扑相可直接用于电路模型的量子计算。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了对称保护拓扑相在现实系统中直接用于通用量子计算的可行性，为基于拓扑有序的可扩展量子计算架构提供了新途径。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological phases have attracted significant interest at the fundamental level and as a potential platform for quantum information processing, owing to their protected edge states and resilience to perturbations. Applying these features for practical and efficient quantum computation is highly desirable, but remains an open challenge. Here, we demonstrate the partitioning into multiple independent Haldane phase subsystems of a single spin-1/2 ladder system and propose this as a scalable architecture for gate-based quantum computation, which takes advantage of the symmetry-protected topological order. We encode qubits in the two topological states of the $S^{z}=0$ sector of each subsystem. Finite-size effects, typically viewed as detrimental, instead provide a controllable energy splitting that enables single-qubit rotations using only local magnetic fields. An Ising-type interaction between neighboring subsystem edges generates entangling gates, enabling universal quantum computation driven by two control parameters that are easily accessible experimentally. Our results demonstrate how symmetry-protected topological phases can be directly harnessed for circuit-model quantum computation in realistic systems.

</details>


### [156] [Measurement Geometry for Quantum Random Access Codes: Beyond Nayak Bound and Toward Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12700)
*Seiseki Akibue,Rudy Raymond,Suguru Tamaki,Kosei Teramoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将量子随机访问码(QRAC)的译码概率重新表述为非对易测量的谱问题，给出了Nayak界的初等证明，并提出了改进的有限尺寸上界。进一步定义了互不偏测量值(MUPVM)作为互不偏基的推广，证明了最优两比特QRAC的译码测量必为MUPVM，并基于MUPVM提出了达到猜想界的(M+2,M)-QRAC族构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Nayak界是QRAC译码概率的通用上界，但数值证据表明在小量子比特区域存在更强的上界，因此需要更精确的上界理论以及对最优方案的刻画。

Method: 将QRAC译码概率重新表述为译码测量的谱问题，通过简化Chernoff界论证给出Nayak界的初等证明，并改进论证获得超越Nayak界的有限尺寸上界。在此基础上定义MUPVM概念并分析其与最优QRAC的关系。

Result: 1) 给出了Nayak界的初等证明；2) 获得了在全有限尺寸下优于Nayak界的上界；3) 证明了任意达到猜想界的双比特QRAC的译码测量必为MUPVM；4) 证明了任意MUPVM辅助一个辅助量子比特可构成达到最优N标度译码概率的QRAC；5) 提出了基于MUPVM的(M+2,M)-QRAC族构造，达到猜想界。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上改进了QRAC的译码概率上界，引入了MUPVM这一有用概念，并提供了新的达到最优的构造方案，推动了QRAC理论的精确理解。

Abstract: Quantum random access codes (QRACs) ask how well N classical bits can be encoded into M qubits while allowing any single bit to be recovered. Although the Nayak bound remains the standard general upper bound on the decoding probability, numerical evidence suggests a stronger upper bound in the small-qubit regime.
  In this work, we formulate the optimal decoding probability in terms of decoding measurements, reformulating QRAC design as a spectral problem for noncommuting measurements. Using this formulation, we give an elementary proof of the Nayak bound by simplifying the Chernoff-bound argument. Moreover, we refine the argument to obtain upper bounds that improve over Nayak's bound in the entire finite-size regime.
  The equality conditions of our bounds justify defining mutually unbiased projector-valued measurements (MUPVMs), a generalization of mutually unbiased bases. We show that decoding measurement of any two-qubit QRAC attaining the conjectured bound must form MUPVMs. We also show that any MUPVM, assisted by one ancillary qubit, yields a QRAC with optimal N-scaling decoding probability. Finally, we propose a new MUPVM-based construction for the (M+2,M)-QRAC family attaining the conjectured bound.

</details>


### [157] [Block algebra for morphing circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12724)
*Rui Chao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种基于CNOT的CSS变形电路构造，通过块代数符号和置换矩阵实现，其中前三种源自表面码和颜色码的变形电路，第四种是基于6.6.6颜色码的三轮新构造，并通过有限群的正则表示进行数值搜索实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形电路是量子纠错中降低硬件需求的新范式，现有构造（如ST25中的双块群代数码）仍有改进空间，需要更系统化的构造方法和明确的量子比特连接度规范。

Method: 采用块代数符号系统，以置换矩阵生成的代数为条目，构建四种CNOT-based CSS变形电路。前三种通过重写表面码和颜色码的现有变形电路获得，第四种基于6.6.6颜色码设计为三轮构造。数值搜索利用有限群的正则表示实例化置换矩阵。

Result: 成功给出四种具有明确量子比特连接度的变形电路构造，包括恢复ST25中双块群代数码的变形电路，并提出了新的三轮6.6.6颜色码构造。

Conclusion: 块代数符号系统为变形电路提供了一种系统化的描述方法，有限群的正则表示可有效实例化置换矩阵，为量子纠错中变形电路的硬件实现提供了更多选择。

Abstract: Morphing circuits are a new paradigm for quantum error correction that relaxes hardware requirements. We present four constructions for CNOT-based CSS morphing circuits with explicit qubit connectivity degrees. All four constructions are specified in block algebra notation, with entries in algebras generated by permutation matrices. The first three are obtained by rewriting existing surface- and color-code morphing circuits; the fourth is a new three-round construction modeled on the 6.6.6 color code. The surface-code construction recovers the morphing circuit of Ref. [ST25] for two-block group algebra codes. Numerical search then instantiates these permutation matrices using regular representations of finite groups.
  [ST25] M. H. Shaw and B. M. Terhal, Phys. Rev. Lett. 134(9), 090602 (2025).

</details>


### [158] [Stable, bidirectional electro-optic transduction in thin film lithium tantalate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12726)
*Christopher J. Axline,Stephan Gamper,Phoebe M. Tengdin,Moritz Businger,Guilhem Alma,Marina A. Roquet,Nicola Brusadin,Robin Giroud,Luis G. Villanueva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在薄膜铌酸锂（TFLT）平台上实现了集成电光微波-光学换能器，展示了高吞吐率晶圆级制造、双向相干转换、数天连续稳定运行以及低附加噪声等关键性能，确立了TFLT作为未来量子互连和模块化量子处理器的可扩展、稳健电光平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
