Motivation: 高效且稳定的微波-光学换能是实现分布式超导量子计算和异构量子网络的关键使能技术。虽然基于薄膜铌酸锂（TFLN）的电光换能器展现出巨大潜力，但现有演示受限于低频偏漂移、低效率、制造复杂性和可扩展性等问题。

Method: 在薄膜铌酸锂（TFLT）平台上，利用深紫外光刻晶圆级工艺制造与可调光子分子光学谐振腔耦合的超导微波谐振腔，实现数百个器件的高吞吐量生产。采用静态偏置场并辅以最小反馈，实现多天连续运行。

Result: 在六个器件上观察到C波段光子与4.9-5.5 GHz微波光子之间的双向相干转换，片上效率与理论一致，单光子耦合率g₀/2π ~ 1 kHz。在100微秒脉冲泵浦下，最高效率处附加噪声小于一个光子，并实现了多天连续稳定运行。

Conclusion: TFLT被确立为一个可扩展且稳健的电光平台，为未来的量子互连和模块化量子处理器提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Efficient and stable microwave-optical transduction is a key enabling technology for distributed superconducting quantum computing and heterogeneous quantum networks. Electro-optic transducers based on thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) have shown strong promise, but demonstrations to date have been limited by various factors such as low frequency bias drift, low efficiency, fabrication complexity, and scalability. Here we demonstrate the first integrated electro-optic microwave-optical transducers realized in thin-film lithium tantalate (TFLT), a material platform offering Pockels nonlinearity comparable to TFLN together with improved bias stability and high-power handling. We fabricate superconducting microwave resonators coupled to tunable photonic-molecule optical resonators using wafer-scale deep ultraviolet lithography, offering high-throughput production of hundreds of devices per wafer. Across six devices we observe coherent bidirectional conversion between C-band optical photons and 4.9-5.5 GHz microwave photons, with measured on-chip efficiencies and inferred single-photon coupling rates g_0/2π ~ 1 kHz consistent with theory. Continuous operation over multiple days is achieved using a static bias field with minimal feedback, demonstrating a major operational advantage. We further characterize optical loss statistics, microwave resonator performance, and optically induced added noise under pulsed pumping, finding less than one added photon for 100 microsecond pulses at the highest measured efficiencies. These results establish TFLT as a scalable and robust electro-optic platform for future quantum interconnects and modular quantum processors.

</details>


### [159] [Certifying Nonclassical Proper-Time Histories with a Quantum Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12755)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子钟可以从运动或引力固有时间差中获得相对论相位，但仅减少退相干不足以认证非经典固有时间历史。本文将这一区分形式化为通道认证问题，证明任何二能级单时间退相干信号（包括有效量子固有时间标签生成的信号）都允许经典随机固有时间表示，并定义了实验指定固有时间历史的经典混合凸集，证明了条件相干历史重组的Choi秩分离准则。双分支Ramsey协议给出了该经典集之外的明暗端口布居数见证者。该认证是操作性的且相对于指定历史集而言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分量子钟从相对论相位中获得的效果是否真正源于非经典固有时间历史，而非可由经典随机过程模拟的现象。

Method: 将问题形式化为通道认证问题，证明单时间退相干信号的经典可表示性，定义经典混合凸集，提出Choi秩分离准则，设计双分支Ramsey协议。

Result: 任何二能级单时间退相干信号允许经典随机固有时间表示；建立了条件相干历史重组的Choi秩分离准则；双分支Ramsey协议可产生经典集之外的明暗端口布居数见证者。

Conclusion: 实现了对非经典固有时间历史的操作性认证，且认证相对于指定的实验固有时间历史集有效，排除了相同历史集的经典混合而非任意经典协议。

Abstract: Quantum clocks can acquire relativistic phases from motional or gravitational proper-time differences, but reduced clock dephasing alone does not certify nonclassical proper-time histories. We formulate this distinction as a channel-certification problem. First, we show that any two-level single-time dephasing signal, including one generated by an effective quantum proper-time label, admits a classical random proper-time representation. We then define the convex set of classical mixtures of experimentally specified proper-time histories and prove a Choi-rank separation criterion for conditioned coherent history recombination. A two-branch Ramsey protocol gives explicit bright- and dark-port population witnesses outside this classical set. The certification is operational and relative to the specified history set: it rules out classical mixtures of the same implemented proper-time histories, not arbitrary classical protocols with different histories or controls.

</details>


### [160] [Instabilities in a Non-KAM System via Information Scrambling: A Note](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12761)
*Naga Dileep Varikuti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非KAM体系（ kicked harmonic oscillator）中，共振对算子增长和OTOC的影响，发现共振时OTOC呈二次时间增长（远离时线性增长），并揭示出由欧拉 totient 函数控制的数论结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典谐振子具有简并性，动力学超出常规KAM框架，共振在相空间中起核心作用。作者希望探索非KAM系统中共振如何影响量子算子增长和信息扩散。

Method: 以 kicked harmonic oscillator 为模型，采用OTOC作为诊断工具，结合微扰理论推导出封闭形式的OTOC表达式，分析共振与非共振条件下的行为差异。

Result: 共振处OTOC呈二次时间增长，而非共振处为线性增长；OTOC行为展现出由频率比的欧拉 totient 函数控制的数论结构。

Conclusion: 共振结构在控制信息扩散中起重要作用，即使在经典Lyapunov指数很小（无常规混沌）的情况下，量子OTOC仍能敏感捕获共振导致的相空间结构变化。

Abstract: We study operator growth in quantized non-KAM systems using out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs), focusing on the kicked harmonic oscillator as a representative example. Since the classical harmonic oscillator is degenerate, the dynamics fall outside the usual Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser (KAM) framework, and resonances play a central role in shaping the phase space. We examine the system near resonances, where the ratio between the oscillator and driving frequencies takes integer values. Even though the classical Lyapunov exponent remains small at these points, and hence no conventional chaos, the phase space still undergoes strong structural changes. The OTOCs are particularly sensitive to these resonances, with a quadratic-in-time growth at resonance compared to linear growth away from it. Within a perturbative treatment, we derive closed-form expressions for the OTOCs and uncover a number-theoretic structure emerging in the behavior of OTOCs, governed by the Euler totient function of the frequency ratio. Overall, the results we present in this short note imply that resonant structures can play an important role in controlling information spreading.

</details>


### [161] [Matrix phase-space representations for quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12769)
*Peter D. Drummond,Alexander S. Dellios,Margaret D. Reid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种称为矩阵相空间（matrix phase-space）的广义相空间表示方法，通过在基展开中融入全局量子对称性并将基投影到约化希尔伯特空间，大幅降低多体量子模拟的采样误差，统一了多种先前的相空间方法。论文提供了基本定理和算符恒等式的详细证明，并针对多种对称性进行了处理。以高斯玻色采样（GBS）量子计算机输出验证这一前沿问题为例，展示了利用宇称对称性可将采样误差相对于传统方法降低极大倍数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相空间方法在量子多体模拟中存在采样误差较大的问题，且不同方法之间缺乏统一的理论框架。将量子对称性显式纳入相空间表示有望突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出矩阵相空间表示法：在基展开中引入全局量子对称性，将原希尔伯特空间投影到对称性约束下的约化子空间，构建新的相空间分布函数与算符对应规则。

Result: 严格证明了矩阵相空间的基本定理与算符恒等式；在GBS验证应用中，利用宇称对称性实现了相比传统方法极大倍数的采样误差降低。

Conclusion: 矩阵相空间方法提供了一种统一且高效的量子模拟框架，对称性约化能显著提升计算精度与效率，尤其适用于具有对称结构的量子系统。

Abstract: We introduce a general phase-space representation that includes global quantum symmetries in the basis expansion. This method, called matrix phase-space, projects the basis onto a reduced Hilbert space, which can greatly reduce sampling errors of many-body quantum simulations and unifies several previous phase-space methods. The purpose of this paper is to provide detailed proofs of basic theorems and operator identities. We also treat several different types of symmetries. To illustrate the benefits of matrix phase-space methods, we give a detailed derivation of a recent application to the topical problem of verifying the outputs of Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) quantum computers with photon number resolving detectors. This has exponential complexity, and using parity symmetry reduces sampling errors by very large factors relative to earlier methods.

</details>


### [162] [Explicit Quantum Circuit Simulation of Nonlinear 1-Dimensional Fluid with Carleman-linearized Boltzmann Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12770)
*Keita Kanno,Kazumasa Ueno,Hayato Higuchi,Morimasa Okamoto,Yuya Yoshizuru,Ryoya Ishimaru,Towa Takagi,Kentaro Sakamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过二阶Carleman线性化将一维玻尔兹曼方程的非线性项纳入量子计算框架，并利用基于量子奇异值变换的泰勒展开ODE求解器，在显式量子电路层面模拟了非线性流体动力学的量子计算。门和量子比特复杂度随网格尺寸呈对数缩放，为工业级量子CFD提供了基线参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算流体动力学（CFD）作为容错量子计算机的关键应用日益受到关注，但此前显式且高效的量子电路模拟仅在线性情况下得到验证，非线性流体动力学的量子计算方案尚待突破。

Method: 采用二阶Carleman线性化对一维玻尔兹曼方程的非线性项进行近似处理，再结合基于量子奇异值变换（QSVT）的泰勒展开ODE求解器，在显式量子电路层面实现最终时刻态的制备。

Result: 门复杂度和量子比特复杂度均随网格尺寸呈对数缩放；高阶Carleman线性化能够捕获更多非线性特征；高阶泰勒展开在ODE求解器中具有实际效用。

Conclusion: 该构造为降低计算成本提供了具体基线，并为未来向更高维度、复杂几何、物理量提取等工业级量子CFD应用拓展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum computation of fluid dynamics has attracted growing attention as a key application of fault-tolerant quantum computers anticipated in the coming decade, with lattice Boltzmann methods emerging as a particularly promising approach. Explicit and efficient elementary-gate-level circuit simulations, however, have so far been demonstrated only in the linear case. Here we include the leading nonlinearity through second-order Carleman linearization of the one-dimensional Boltzmann equation, and demonstrate, via explicit quantum-circuit simulation, the preparation of the final-time state using a Taylor-expansion-based ODE solver based on the quantum singular value transformation. With this construction, we analyze the gate and qubit complexities, which scale logarithmically with the grid size, the nonlinearity captured by the higher-order Carleman linearization, and the practical utility of higher-order expansions in the Taylor ODE solver. The construction provides a concrete baseline for computational cost reduction and further developments such as extensions to higher dimensions, complex geometries, and the extraction of physical quantities, towards industrially useful quantum CFD.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum Network Routing based on Surface Code Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12781)
*Tianjie Hu,Jindi Wu,Qun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SurfNet是一种结合量子纠缠网络和直接传输网络优势的新型量子网络架构，利用表面码作为逻辑量子位进行消息编码，并通过两条并行通信通道以模块化方式容错传输每个表面码。仿真结果表明，该方法能有效提升通信保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络面临信道噪声和擦除误差的严峻挑战，现有两类网络（基于量子纠缠的隐形传态网络和直接传输量子消息的网络）各自存在局限，难以同时实现高可靠性和高效率。

Method: 1) 采用表面码作为逻辑量子位编码消息；2) 利用两条并行通信通道以模块化方式容错传输表面码；3) 提出新型网络架构以更好地将表面码集成到量子网络中；4) 提出一种新型纠错解码器，充分利用表面码在网络中的模块化特性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SurfNet及其解码器显著提升了量子网络内的通信保真度。

Conclusion: SurfNet通过融合两类量子网络的优点，结合表面码纠错和双通道并行传输，有效解决了量子网络中的噪声和擦除误差问题，实现了高可靠性与高效率的统一。

Abstract: Quantum networks encounter unavoidable channel noises and erasure errors, presenting a huge obstacle in designing protocols that attain both high reliability and efficiency. Typically, quantum networks fall into two categories: those utilize quantum entanglements for quantum teleportation, and those directly transfer the actual quantum messages. In this paper, we present SurfNet, a quantum network that inherits the main advantages from both categories. It employs surface codes as logical qubits for encoding messages, and utilizes two parallel communication channels to fault-tolerantly transfer each surface code in a modular manner. Our approach of using surface codes can timely correct both operational and photon loss errors within the network, and the integration of the two channels within the network can greatly improve network throughput. For the implementation of SurfNet, we propose a novel network architecture, designed to better integrate surface codes into quantum networks. We also propose a novel error correction decoder, designed to fully utilize the modular characteristic of surface codes within our network. Simulation results demonstrate that SurfNet with its decoder significantly enhances the communication fidelity within quantum networks.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond-Third-Order Quantum Coherence in Two-Dimensional Spectroscopy via Order-Selective Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12794)
*Xue Zhang,De-Ran Zhang,Hui Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合旋转坐标系采集与帧移追踪算法的计算辅助策略，在二维光谱中成功分离7阶非线性信号与3阶背景，无需相位循环即可读取高阶量子相干动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性光谱中高阶弱信号被低阶强信号光谱重叠掩盖，传统相位循环方案扩展到高阶时测量与分析复杂度急剧上升，亟需一种简便实用方法来解耦不同阶次的非线性响应。

Method: 采用旋转坐标系采集策略，结合帧移追踪（Frame-Shift Tracking）算法，利用信号在不同帧下的光谱频移差异来实现阶次选择性分离。

Result: 在铷蒸气实验中，成功从共存的3阶信号中分离出7阶非线性贡献，并直接观测到高阶量子相干动力学，且可在较高脉冲强度下运行。

Conclusion: 该方法与多维光谱平台高度兼容，为超越三阶的集体与多体超快动力学探测提供了实用途径。

Abstract: A central challenge in nonlinear spectroscopy is the order-selective readout of weak higher-order responses that spectrally overlap with dominant lower-order signals. This bottleneck is particularly severe in two-dimensional (2D) spectroscopy, where extending conventional phase-cycling schemes to higher orders rapidly increases measurement and analysis complexity. Here we introduce a computation-assisted strategy that combines rotating-frame acquisition with a frame-shift tracking algorithm to separate signals by their frame-dependent spectral shifts. In a rubidium vapor experiment, we use this approach to isolate a 7th-order nonlinear contribution from coexisting 3rd-order components, enabling direct access to higher-order quantum-coherence dynamics without sacrificing operation at comparatively high pulse intensities. The method is broadly compatible with multidimensional spectroscopy platforms and provides a practical route to probing many-body and collective ultrafast dynamics beyond third order.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum Reservoir Computing for Short-Term Power Load Forecasting in Resource-Constrained Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12806)
*Mansi Od,Param Pathak,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件高效的量子储层计算（QRC）框架用于短期负荷预测，通过固定量子储层结合经典弹性网络读出，并对读出进行8至2比特的后训练定点量化压缩。在Tetouan和Spain数据集上，6比特精度可保持全精度性能，读出内存减少81.2%，且训练后的读出可直接迁移至含噪声的量子储层状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的短期负荷预测需要模型在有限内存、有限测量预算和硬件噪声条件下保持准确，现有方法难以同时满足这些约束。

Method: 采用固定量子储层将时序输入窗口映射到高维特征空间，仅训练经典弹性网络读出；训练后通过定点量化将读出压缩至8到2比特宽度。

Result: 6比特读出精度可媲美全精度性能，内存减少81.2%；低于该阈值时退化程度因数据集而异（Tetouan更敏感，Spain更平缓）；硬件噪声验证表明训练好的读出可直接迁移至噪声储层。

Conclusion: 量化QRC是一种面向近期待量子时序应用、资源感知的预测方法，适用于实际部署中的边缘设备场景。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting is essential for reliable energy management, but practical deployment on edge devices requires models that remain accurate under limited memory, finite measurement budgets, and hardware noise. This work proposes a hardware-efficient Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) framework for energy load forecasting, where a fixed quantum reservoir transforms temporal input windows into high-dimensional features and only a classical Elastic Net readout is trained. To reduce deployment cost, the trained readout is compressed using post-training fixed-point quantization at bit widths from 8 to 2 bits. The framework is evaluated on the Tetouan and Spain energy load datasets under exact statevector simulation, 512-shot finite sampling, and realistic hardware-noise models from IBM FakeTorino and IBM FakeMarrakesh. Results show that 6-bit readout precision preserves full-precision forecasting performance while reducing readout memory by 81.2%. Below this point, degradation becomes dataset dependent, with Tetouan showing stronger sensitivity and Spain degrading more gradually. Hardware-noise validation further shows that the trained readout transfers to noisy reservoir states without retraining. These findings support quantized QRC as a resource-aware forecasting approach for near-term quantum time-series applications.

</details>


### [166] [Accidental Symmetry in the Tavis-Cummings Model via the Schwinger Boson Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12813)
*Plato Deliyannis,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Tavis-Cummings（TC）哈密顿量中存在一个额外的“偶然”对称性，该对称性对n>2量子比特的可实现的幺正变换施加了强约束，并利用Schwinger玻色子表示解释了其起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TC模型通常被认为具有置换不变性和U(1)对称性，但作者发现存在一个先前未被注意的额外对称性，这对量子计算和控制有重要影响。

Method: 通过理论分析识别TC哈密顿量的新对称性，构建对应的守恒可观测量，使用Schwinger玻色子表示解释对称性起源，并研究J_z和J_z^2项对该对称性的影响。

Result: 发现对于n>2量子比特，该对称性对可实现幺正变换施加强约束；该约束在存在全局J_z哈密顿量时依然存在，但加入J_z^2项后可被移除。

Conclusion: 这一新发现的对称性对TC系统的可控性及其在量子计算中的应用有重要限制，相关影响在姊妹论文中进一步研究。

Abstract: The Jaynes-Cummings (JC) Hamiltonian is a paradigmatic model of light-matter interaction and, more generally, qubit-boson interactions, widely used across atomic, optical, and superconducting qubit platforms. In the multi-qubit setting, where n qubits are identically coupled to a single boson mode, this interaction is known as the Tavis-Cummings (TC) Hamiltonian. The structure of the TC model is usually understood in terms of two standard symmetries: permutation invariance of the qubits and a U(1) symmetry associated with conservation of the total excitation number. Here we identify an additional, independent "accidental" symmetry of the TC Hamiltonian and construct the corresponding conserved observable. We show that, for n>2 qubits, this symmetry imposes strong constraints on the realizable unitary transformations. These constraints persist in the presence of the global $J_z$ Hamiltonian, but are removed by adding $J_z^2$, even though $J_z^2$ preserves both permutation invariance and the U(1) symmetry. Finally, we explain the origin of this previously unnoticed symmetry using Schwinger's boson representation of angular momentum. These restrictions have important implications for controllability of the TC system and for its applications to quantum computing, which are investigated further in a companion paper.

</details>


### [167] [Graph Reinforcement Learning for Calibration-Aware Quantum Circuit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12816)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图强化学习的校准感知量子电路路由器，利用IBM Heron r2的当日校准数据选择硬件边SWAP操作。在9个MQT Bench电路和3个校准快照上的评估显示，该方法平均保真度达到0.727，显著优于SABRE-best20的0.440和目标感知SABRE的0.481，但保真度提升主要集中于5q和8q电路，且以更高的双量子门数量为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子电路路由方法仅关注门计数等开销指标，忽略了经过校准不良的耦合器时保真度下降的问题，因此需要一种校准感知的路由策略来提升实际执行保真度。

Method: 使用图强化学习（图神经网络+近端策略优化PPO）作为路由器，利用IBM Heron r2的当日校准数据作为奖励信号，通过选择硬件边SWAP操作来路由量子电路。

Result: 在9个MQT Bench电路和3个校准快照上，方法平均保真度0.727，高于SABRE-best20的0.440和目标感知SABRE的0.481；保真度提升集中在5q和8q电路，所有10q电路仍以SABRE-best20为优。

Conclusion: 校准感知的强化学习路由可以在门计数驱动的编译之外进一步提升保真度，但当前方法在较大电路上仍存在局限。

Abstract: Quantum circuit routing is a key step in compiling programs for noisy intermediate-scale quantum processors. Routes that appear efficient by standard overhead metrics can still lose fidelity when they pass through poorly calibrated couplers. We study a calibration-aware graph reinforcement-learning router that uses same-day IBM Heron r2 calibration data to choose hardware-edge SWAPs. We train the policy with proximal policy optimization and evaluate it with exact simulated fidelity across nine Munich Quantum Toolkit (MQT) Bench circuits and three calibration snapshots. Across these evaluations, pooled mean exact fidelity is $0.727$, compared with $0.440$ for SABRE-best20 and $0.481$ for target-aware SABRE. Fidelity gains come with higher routed two-qubit counts and are concentrated in the 5q and 8q circuit families; under the fixed tree action graph, all 10q families favor SABRE-best20. Overall, our results show that calibration-aware learned routing can improve fidelity beyond gate-count-driven compilation.

</details>


### [168] [Quantum walk-based optimisation for capacitated vehicle routing with homogeneous and heterogeneous fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12856)
*Edric Matwiejew,Aidan Smith,Callum Neill,Paulo Santos,Ugo Varetto,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子行走的优化算法(QWOA)用于解决带容量约束的车辆路径问题(CVRP)，通过在乘积空间上执行连续时间量子行走，将每层门复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(n^{3}\log n)$降至$\mathcal{O}(n^{2}\log n)$，仿真实验表明在最多8个客户和3辆车的实例上能以更少的目标函数评估实现更优收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVRP因其组合复杂性和实际重要性成为量子优化的理想候选，但其约束搜索空间对量子算法构成挑战，现有QWOA方法门复杂度过高。

Method: 提出QWOA算法，在乘积空间上执行连续时间量子行走，该乘积空间与CVRP解空间的组合结构自然对应；支持由固定数量经典参数生成的电路参数化调度。

Result: 每层门复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(n^{3}\log n)$降至$\mathcal{O}(n^{2}\log n)$；对最多8客户3车辆的实例进行精确态矢量仿真，证明能以更少目标函数评估实现更优收敛，且优势随问题规模增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 结构化的乘积空间量子行走是约束组合空间优化的一种有前景的工具。

Abstract: The capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP) is an appealing candidate for quantum optimisation due to its combinatorial complexity and practical importance. However, the problem's constrained search space poses a challenge for such quantum algorithms. We introduce a quantum walk-based optimisation algorithm (QWOA) for the CVRP with homogeneous or heterogeneous vehicle fleets, addressing this challenge through a continuous-time quantum walk over a product space that coincides with combinatorial structures intrinsic to the CVRP solution space. Relative to the prior QWOA-based formulation, this approach reduces the per-layer gate complexity from $\mathcal{O}(n^{3}\log n)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n^{2}\log n)$ and supports a circuit parameterisation schedule generated by a fixed number of classical parameters. Exact state-vector simulation on instances with up to $n=8$ customers and $K=3$ vehicles demonstrates improved convergence to low-cost solutions using markedly fewer objective function evaluations, with the advantage broadening as problem size increases. These results identify structured product-space walks as a promising tool for optimisation over constrained combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [169] [Quantum Otto engine powered by an anisotropic Heisenberg XYZ model under independent local magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12877)
*Meiru Li,Maimaitiyiming Tusun,Fang Zhao,Hasiyatihan Abudoula,Tongcheng Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一个由各向异性两量子比特Heisenberg XYZ模型构成的工作物质在量子奥托热机中的表现。通过独立局域磁场控制每个自旋，系统分析了纵向耦合、各向异性、横向耦合和局域磁场对净功输出和效率的影响。发现减小纵向耦合可显著提高最大功和峰值效率，各向异性参数存在最优值。局域功分析揭示了循环中功的产生机制，由于不对称局域磁场和自旋-自旋相互作用，两个量子比特扮演了截然不同的热力学角色，相互作用项本身对总功有重要贡献。进一步分析了循环中纠缠度（concurrence）的变化，结果表明热等磁冲程和冷等磁冲程间纠缠度的显著变化与效率提升密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究各向异性两量子比特Heisenberg XYZ模型在量子奥托热机中的热力学性能，探索不同参数（纵向耦合、各向异性、横向耦合、局域磁场）对净功输出和效率的影响，并揭示量子纠缠在循环过程中的作用。

Method: 以各向异性两量子比特Heisenberg XYZ模型为工作物质，采用独立局域磁场分别控制每个自旋，构建量子奥托热机循环。通过系统扫描纵向耦合、各向异性参数、横向耦合和局域磁场等参数，计算净功输出和效率，并进行局域功分析和纠缠度（concurrence）的演化分析。

Result: 减小纵向耦合显著提升最大功和峰值效率；各向异性参数存在最优值；由于不对称局域磁场和自旋-自旋相互作用，两量子比特的热力学角色不同，相互作用项对总功有重要贡献；冷热等磁冲程间纠缠度的显著变化与效率提升密切相关。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子奥托热机的运行原理和控制提供了新的见解，揭示了量子纠缠在热力学循环中的关键作用以及各参数对性能的调控规律。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto heat engine whose working substance is an anisotropic two-qubit Heisenberg XYZ model. Independent local magnetic fields are used to control each spin individually. The influence of the longitudinal coupling, anisotropy, transverse coupling, and local fields on the net work output and efficiency is systematically examined. Reducing the longitudinal coupling is found to markedly improve both the maximum work and the peak efficiency. The engine performance reaches an optimum at a particular value of the anisotropy parameter. A local work analysis clarifies how work is produced during the cycle. Because of the asymmetric local fields and the intrinsic spin-spin interaction, the two qubits play markedly different thermodynamic roles; the interaction term itself contributes crucially to the total work. We further analyze the variation of quantum entanglement, quantified by concurrence, along the cycle. The results indicate that a pronounced change in entanglement between the hot and cold isomagnetic strokes is closely correlated with the efficiency enhancement. This work offers new insight into the operating principles and control of quantum Otto heat engines.

</details>


### [170] [Global Control with the Tavis-Cummings Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12906)
*Plato Deliyannis,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在全局控制下，多个量子比特通过Tavis-Cummings（TC）相互作用与单个玻色模耦合时的系统可控性。发现对于n>2个量子比特，可实现酉算子受到TC哈密顿量中一种“意外”对称性的限制，而引入$J_z^2$哈密顿量可打破该对称性，从而实现半普适性：即能够实现满足置换对称性和U(1)对称性的任意酉算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在全局控制（所有量子比特接受相同控制脉冲）和集体耦合（通过TC相互作用与同一玻色模耦合）条件下，量子比特-玻色模联合系统的可控性，探索哪些酉变换可以在该系统中实现。

Method: 通过理论分析TC哈密顿量及其对称性结构，研究TC相互作用与全局$J_z$场联合生成的酉算子群，识别出除标准U(1)和置换对称性外的一种“意外”对称性，并引入$J_z^2$哈密顿量来打破该对称性，分析其可控性。

Result: 对于n>2个量子比特，TC+$J_z$能实现的酉算子受意外对称性限制；加入$J_z^2$后，系统实现半普适性，可生成任意满足置换和U(1)对称性的酉算子（受群中心约束）。

Conclusion: 全局控制下的TC系统具有非平凡的可控性结构，意外对称性限制了其实现能力，而$J_z^2$可恢复部分普适性，为量子计算中的全局控制方案提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the controllability of a system of qubits under global control, where control pulses act identically on all qubits. Specifically, we consider a collection of qubits identically coupled to a single bosonic mode, or harmonic oscillator, via the Jaynes-Cummings interaction. This collective coupling, known as the Tavis-Cummings (TC) interaction, has been realized in several quantum computing platforms, including superconducting and atomic qubit systems. Although the qubits do not interact directly with one another, they can become entangled through their common coupling to the bosonic mode. We characterize the group of unitaries that can be implemented on the joint Hilbert space of the qubits and bosonic mode using the TC interaction together with a global $z$ field $J_z$, corresponding to identical z rotations on all qubits. We show that for n>2 qubits the set of realizable unitaries is restricted by an "accidental" symmetry of the TC Hamiltonian, distinct from its "standard" U(1) and permutational symmetries. On the other hand, we find that the Hamiltonian $J_z^2$ breaks this accidental symmetry and, together with the TC interaction and $J_z$, achieves semi-universality: it allows the implementation of arbitrary unitaries that respect permutational and U(1) symmetry, up to certain constraints on the center of the group. In a companion paper, we further analyze this remarkable accidental symmetry and show that it can be understood through Schwinger's bosonic model of angular momentum.

</details>


### [171] [Non-Hermitian skin effect induced by spatial noncommutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12961)
*Zheng Wei,Su-Peng Kou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出“非交换趋肤效应”：当空间坐标不对易时，沿一个方向施加的增益-损耗势会自动在另一方向产生非互易性，导致所有本征态在边界处指数堆积。该效应无需人为引入非厄米参数（如非对称跳跃），逆趋肤长度由不对易参数解析给出，并且可通过正弦虚势的集体翻转实现精确测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非厄米趋肤效应方案均需沿趋肤方向人为引入非对称跳跃或增益损耗等非厄米参数。本文旨在证明当空间坐标不对易时，这种人为引入不再是必需的——沿一个坐标施加的增益-损耗势可通过坐标的非对易性自动生成另一方向的非互易性，从而产生趋肤效应。

Method: 1) 构建坐标不对易条件下的非厄米模型，推导逆趋肤长度的解析公式（热力学极限下严格成立）；2) 通过晶格模型精确对角化进行数值验证；3) 分析虚势的反射对称性作为趋肤效应存在与否的精确判据；4) 研究正弦虚势下本征态集体翻转行为，提出基于翻转点的零交叉测量方案。

Result: 1) 坐标不对易性可自发产生非厄米趋肤效应，逆趋肤长度与不对易参数成正比并由解析公式给出；2) 反射对称性提供了有限尺寸系统下趋肤效应存在性的精确判据；3) 正弦虚势下所有本征态在由几何唯一确定的参数点处集体翻转趋肤方向；4) 翻转点与势强度无关，构成抗系统误差的零交叉测量方案，可直接提取不对易参数。

Conclusion: 非交换趋肤效应提供了一种无需人为引入非厄米参数的新型趋肤机制，可作为空间非对易性的非微扰探针。其晶格模型的Peierls相结构在冷原子合成维度、光子谐振器和拓扑电路中原则上可实现。

Abstract: In all known schemes for the non-Hermitian skin effect, the non-Hermitian ingredient that drives the skin localization, whether asymmetric hopping or gain and loss, is invariably introduced by hand as an independent model parameter along the skin direction. Here we show that when two spatial coordinates do not commute, the skin effect can break free of this paradigm: a gain-loss potential applied along one coordinate automatically generates non-reciprocity along the other through the coordinate noncommutativity, driving all eigenstates to pile up exponentially at a boundary. We term this phenomenon the noncommutative skin effect. The inverse skin length is proportional to the noncommutativity parameter and is given by an analytic formula, exact in the thermodynamic limit and verified by exact diagonalization of lattice models; the reflection symmetry of the imaginary potential furnishes an exact criterion for the presence or absence of the effect, valid rigorously for finite-size systems. For a sinusoidal imaginary potential, the skin direction of all eigenstates flips collectively at parameter points fixed purely by geometry. Because the flip point is independent of the potential strength, the reversal constitutes a zero-crossing measurement scheme intrinsically robust against systematic errors, from which the noncommutativity parameter can be extracted directly. The qualitative transition of the eigenstates from uniform to exponentially localized renders the effect a nonperturbative probe of spatial noncommutativity, and the Peierls-phase structure of its lattice model is in principle accessible to cold-atom synthetic dimensions, photonic resonators, and topolectrical circuits.

</details>


### [172] [Quantum-Driven Neuromorphic Computing for Million-Qubit-Scale Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12968)
*Adams Ivanov,Samer Rahmeh,Erick Giovani Sperandio Nascimento,Daniela Herrmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Apollo是一款基于p-qubit（概率量子比特）的10,000节点神经形态处理器，采用16nm混合信号CMOS工艺，在室温下运行，功耗约0.5W。它通过集成量子熵源实现超快随机转换，结合Hyperion 256互连拓扑高效嵌入密集Ising/QUBO问题，并通过Suzuki-Trotter对应复现横向场量子退火的关键特性，无需低温冷却。在三维自旋玻璃基准测试中，Apollo达到了比低温量子退火硬件更低的基态能量，为室温可扩展的量子驱动能量优化和概率计算平台奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子退火硬件依赖低温冷却、长寿命相干性和微波控制，成本高昂且可扩展性受限。作者旨在开发一种在室温下运行、具备工业可扩展性的概率计算平台，能够复现量子退火的关键动力学特性，同时避免传统量子硬件的苛刻运行条件。

Method: 核心元件p-qubit是一种双稳态随机单元，其连续时间状态波动由集成的量子熵单元（注入真实量子衍生随机性）驱动，实现低能量下的超快随机转换。采用Hyperion 256互连拓扑结构，支持高密度Ising和QUBO问题的嵌入。通过Suzuki-Trotter对应关系，使p-qubit网络的平衡统计和退火动力学复现横向场量子退火的关键特性。采用16nm混合信号CMOS工艺制造10,000节点芯片。

Result: 在300个无序实现的三维自旋玻璃基准测试中，Apollo达到了比已报道的低温量子退火硬件更低的基态能量，且其性能与经典模拟退火和模拟量子退火存在明显区别。典型模拟核心功耗约0.5W。350nm验证器件实验确认了核心p-qubit动力学、热力学采样正确性和连续时间退火行为。

Conclusion: Apollo被确立为一个室温运行、工业可扩展的平台，适用于量子驱动的能量优化、概率推理、生成建模和混合经典-量子工作流。

Abstract: We introduce Apollo, a 10000 node p-qubit neuromorphic processor fabricated in 16 nm mixed signal CMOS and operating fully at room temperature with a typical analog core power envelope of about 0.5 W. Its fundamental element, the p-qubit, is a bistable stochastic unit whose continuous time state fluctuations are driven by integrated quantum entropy units that inject true quantum derived randomness. This enables ultrafast stochastic transitions at low energy while preserving a classical state representation. Apollo combines these p-qubits with a high degree Hyperion 256 interconnect topology, allowing efficient embedding of dense Ising and QUBO problems with substantially reduced minor embedding overhead compared with sparse annealing platforms. We show that, through the Suzuki Trotter correspondence, the equilibrium statistics and annealing dynamics of the p-qubit network reproduce key properties of transverse field quantum annealing without cryogenic cooling, long lived coherence, or microwave control. Beyond device level validation, Apollo is evaluated on a three dimensional spin glass benchmark previously used to study quantum advantage in superconducting annealers. Across 300 disorder realizations, Apollo reaches substantially lower ground state energies than reported cryogenic quantum annealing hardware, while remaining distinct from classical simulated annealing and simulated quantum annealing. A 350 nm release candidate device experimentally validates the core p-qubit dynamics, thermodynamic sampling correctness, and continuous time annealing behavior. These results establish Apollo as a room temperature, industrially scalable platform for quantum driven energy based optimization, probabilistic inference, generative modeling, and hybrid classical quantum workflows.

</details>


### [173] [Experiment-compatible measurement--feedback quantum state preparation with reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13005)
*Xiaotian Nie,Tao Zhang,Linghui Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分可观测强化学习的自适应测量-反馈协议，仅利用实验可及的测量结果历史来选择测量算符和反馈动作，并引入基于随机采样哈密顿量分量的单次测量的随机终端奖励来避免全态重构，在Bose-Hubbard模型基态制备和GHZ态生成中展示了可扩展性和硬件兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态制备是量子模拟和量子计算中的关键任务，但现有方案要么依赖手工设计的任务特定策略，要么需要全量子态信息——后者在真实实验中不可获取且对大多体系统不实用。

Method: 基于部分可观测强化学习的自适应测量-反馈协议：控制器仅使用实验可及的测量结果历史来选择测量算符和反馈动作；引入基于随机采样哈密顿量分量的单次测量的随机终端奖励，作为目标能量的无偏估计器，避免非物理的全态重构。

Result: 在Bose-Hubbard模型基态制备和GHZ态生成中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了一条可扩展且硬件兼容的量子态制备路径。

Abstract: Ground-state preparation is a critical task in quantum simulation and quantum computing, as it enables the study of correlated phases and the generation of entangled resource states. While measurement--feedback control has emerged as a promising route to state preparation, existing schemes either rely on handcrafted, task-specific policies or are designed using full quantum-state information that is unavailable in real experiments and becomes impractical for large many-body systems. Here we develop an adaptive measurement--feedback protocol based on reinforcement learning under partial observability. The controller uses only the history of experimentally accessible measurement outcomes to choose both the measurement operator and the feedback action in real time. To make training compatible with experiments, we introduce a stochastic terminal reward built from one-shot measurements of randomly sampled Hamiltonian components, avoiding unphysical full-state reconstruction while remaining an unbiased estimator of the target energy. We demonstrate the method by preparing ground states of the Bose--Hubbard model and by generating GHZ states, establishing a scalable and hardware-compatible route to quantum state preparation.

</details>


### [174] [QuBE/Qubex: an integrated hardware-software system for superconducting qubit experiments with broadband control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13010)
*Akinori Machino,Kazuhisa Ogawa,Takefumi Miyoshi,Hidehisa Shiomi,Shinichi Morisaka,Ryo Matsuda,Nilton F. G. Filho,Koichiro Ban,Takafumi Miyanaga,Keisuke Koike,Ryutaro Ohira,Toshi Sumida,Yoshinori Kurimoto,Yuuya Sugita,Yosuke Ito,Yasunari Suzuki,Peter A. Spring,Shiyu Wang,Hiroto Mukai,Arvind Mamgain,Shuhei Tamate,Yutaka Tabuchi,Yasunobu Nakamura,Makoto Negoro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种集成的超导量子比特控制系统，结合宽带微波硬件（瞬时覆盖1.6 GHz）和脉冲级软件栈，在64量子比特固定频率transmon芯片上实现了全芯片频率识别、双量子比特门保真度98.34%及多能级读取等验证，并以开源形式提供了可检视的硬件-软件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超导量子比特系统的高保真度运行需要兼具宽频覆盖、低串扰、紧同步的控制硬件，以及协调系统配置、实验执行和数据分析的软件。现有方案在集成度与可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: 硬件方面采用宽带微波架构，控制输出瞬时带宽达1.6 GHz；软件方面构建脉冲级软件栈，通过自动化配置和内建实验工作流降低校准开销。系统在64量子比特固定频率transmon芯片上验证。

Result: 全芯片频率识别成功；多单元远失谐交叉共振校准与基准测试测得双量子比特门保真度98.34%；实现了计算子空间外的多能级读取。

Conclusion: 通过公开硬件架构并以开源形式发布软件栈，该工作为可扩展的超导量子比特控制实验提供了一套可检视的硬件-软件基础。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity operation in large-scale superconducting qubit systems requires not only control hardware with broad frequency coverage, low crosstalk, and tight synchronization but also software that coordinates system configuration, experiment execution, and data analysis. Here we present an integrated qubit-control system that combines broadband microwave hardware with a pulse-level software stack for scalable superconducting qubit experiments. The hardware provides broadband microwave coverage, including an instantaneous span of up to 1.6 GHz from a control output, while the software reduces setup and calibration overhead through automated configuration and built-in experiment workflows. We validate the system on a 64-qubit fixed-frequency transmon chip through full-chip frequency identification and representative demonstrations, including multi-unit far-detuned cross-resonance calibration and benchmarking that yields a measured two-qubit gate fidelity of 98.34%, and multilevel readout beyond the computational subspace. By disclosing the hardware architecture and releasing the software stack as open source, this work provides an inspectable hardware-software foundation for scalable superconducting qubit control experiments.

</details>


### [175] [A Quantum Algorithm for Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13034)
*Aastha Kataria,Devansh Desai,Ashwini Dalvi,Sagar Korde,Abhijeet Pasi,Irfan N A Siddavatam,Sudhir Ranjan Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子随机数生成算法，通过QFT、受控相位旋转和Grover扩散算子的集成，在洗牌混合时间上实现相对于经典马尔可夫链的二次加速（O(√(n log n)) vs O(n log n)），并扩展到qudit系统进一步降低电路深度，在IBM超导硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫链混合（如top-to-random洗牌）的混合时间为O(n log n)，量子算法有望通过量子并行性和振幅放大实现加速。

Method: 集成三种量子原语：(1) QFT对角化马尔可夫转移算子；(2) 受控相位旋转编码特征值谱；(3) Grover扩散算子作为量子版强均匀停时。扩展到qudit系统编码N=d^m个态空间。

Result: 量子算法混合时间O(√(n log n))，qudit扩展进一步降至O(√(log_d N))，QFT电路深度从O(log² N)降至O(log_d² N)。

Conclusion: 该算法实现了对经典马尔可夫链混合的二次加速，且在超导量子硬件上得到实验验证，为量子随机数生成提供了理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for random number generation that achieves a provable quadratic speedup over classical Markov chain mixing, building on the Diaconis-Shahshahani Fourier analysis of the top-to-random card shuffle. The algorithm integrates three quantum primitives into a unified mixing circuit: the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), which diagonalizes the Markov transition operator; controlled phase rotations, which encode the shuffle eigenvalue spectrum; and the Grover diffusion operator, which acts as a quantum analogue of the Aldous-Diaconis strong uniform stopping time by reflecting amplitudes about their mean at each iteration. For an n-qubit register, the mixing time is O(\sqrt{n \log n}) iterations. Extending to m qudits of local dimension d reduces this to O(\sqrt{\log_d N}) iterations, where N = d^m, compared to the classical O(n \log n) bound. The qudit formulation further reduces QFT circuit depth from O(\log^2 N) to O(\log_d^2 N) gates per layer by encoding the same N-state space using m = \log_d N subsystems instead of \log_2 N qubits. We validate both variants on IBM superconducting hardware.

</details>


### [176] [Simple analytical flux-tuned iSWAP pulses for leakage suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13052)
*Dimitrios Georgiadis,Boxi Li,Asier Galicia,Rami Barends,F. A. Cárdenas-López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Φ-DRAG的解析通量控制方法，用于抑制超导量子比特中可调耦合器双量子比特门的泄漏，在15纳秒范围内实现保真度高于99.99%的快速纠缠门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可调耦合器架构通过通量控制实现高开关比的纠缠门，但快速通量调制会导致非绝热跃迁和布居泄漏到非计算态，限制了门性能。需要一种有效抑制泄漏的方法来实现快速、高保真度的双量子比特门。

Method: 提出Φ-DRAG（derivative removal by adiabatic gate）解析通量控制方法，与传统的微波DRAG实现有本质区别，推导了改进的通量调制协议以抑制泄漏。

Result: 该方法将泄漏抑制到10⁻⁴以下（即低于0.01%），在15纳秒范围内实现快速纠缠门，且对量子比特非谐性的不对称性和不同电路参数具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Φ-DRAG方法有效解决了可调耦合器架构中快速通量调制导致的泄漏问题，为实现快速、高保真度的容错量子计算提供了关键技术支持。

Abstract: Fast, high-fidelity two-qubit gates are a key requirement for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Tunable coupler architectures provide a flexible approach for implementing entangling gates through flux control with large on-off ratios, but fast flux modulation can induce diabatic transitions and population leakage to non-computational states, limiting gate performance. Here we present an analytical flux control method enabling derivative removal by adiabatic gate ($Φ$-DRAG) for suppressing leakage in flux tunable two-qubit gates. We show that $Φ$-DRAG differs fundamentally from conventional microwave implementations and derive modified flux modulation protocols that suppress leakage below $10^{-4}$ for fast entangling gates. The method remains effective across a range of asymmetry between qubit anharmonicities and different circuit parameters, enabling high-fidelity two-qubit gates within the fifteen nanosecond range.

</details>


### [177] [Fibonacci Steady-States and Persistent Oscillations in an Ordered Multimode Dicke Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13072)
*Miriam J. Leonhardt,Kai Müller,Oliver Lunt,Andrew J. Daley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有有序最近邻耦合几何的多模Dicke模型的稳定稳态，发现超辐射相中平均场稳定稳态数量呈现斐波那契标度，且部分稳态表现出持续振荡。通过截断Wigner近似和纯态层次方法，验证了这些特征在有限团簇尺寸下仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模Dicke模型此前多聚焦于无序耦合（如自旋玻璃和联想记忆），而有序耦合几何（如最近邻耦合）的性质尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究有序最近邻耦合多模Dicke模型中的稳定稳态行为。

Method: 采用平均场理论推导稳定稳态的标度规律，结合截断Wigner近似（TWA）和数值精确的纯态层次方法（HPS）验证有限尺寸效应。

Result: （1）超辐射相中平均场稳定稳态数量随原子团簇数呈斐波那契标度；（2）部分稳态展现出持续振荡行为；（3）有限团簇尺寸下这些特征仍然存在。

Conclusion: 有序多模Dicke模型（如最近邻耦合几何）在当前实验技术下可实现，并指向一类具有丰富行为的强相互作用耗散系统，拓展了多模腔量子电动力学的研究范畴。

Abstract: Ultracold atoms in multimode optical cavities provide a rich testbed for many-body phenomena enabled by light-mediated interactions. Recent experiments include realizations of spin glasses and associative memories, as described by multimode Dicke models with disordered couplings. However, the properties of multimode Dicke models with ordered coupling geometries remain largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the stable steady-states of the multimode Dicke model with an ordered nearest-neighbor coupling geometry, where $n_c$ atomic clusters are coupled via $n_c-1$ cavity modes. We show that the number of mean-field stable steady-states in the superradiant phase exhibits Fibonacci scaling with the number of atomic clusters, and that a subset of these steady-states exhibit persistent oscillations. Using both the truncated Wigner approximation and the numerically-exact hierarchy of pure states, we further demonstrate that these features of the stable steady-state solutions persist for finite cluster sizes. Ordered multimode Dicke models, such as the nearest-neighbor coupling geometry considered here, are accessible with current experimental technologies and point toward a broader class of strongly interacting dissipative systems with similarly rich behavior.

</details>


### [178] [Hamiltonian-Aware ADAPT Variational Quantum Eigensolver for Molecular Ground-State Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13118)
*Runhong He,Chao Liu,Xin Hong,Qiaozhen Chai,Junyuan Zhou,Ji Guan,Guolong Cui,Shenggang Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿感知自适应VQE算法(HA-ADAPT-VQE)，通过引入基于哈密顿信息的算子选择准则和问题自适应的冗余算子剪枝方法，解决了ADAPT-VQE中算子选择不当和退化算子积累问题，在强关联分子系统中实现了更优的能级精度、更小的ansatz规模和更低的测量成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADAPT-VQE方法存在两个关键问题：不恰当的操作符选择和退化操作符的积累，导致能量平台现象和计算效率低下，亟需一种更鲁棒、高效的ansatz构建方法。

Method: 1) 提出哈密顿感知的激发算子选择准则，通过融入哈密顿量信息打破局部约束，优先选择物理上有意义的激发算子，且不增加额外计算开销；2) 开发问题自适应的冗余算子判别与剪枝方法，平衡冗余算子剪枝与收敛保障，适用于任意规模的ansatz。

Result: 在典型强关联分子系统上的系统数值实验表明，HA-ADAPT-VQE避免了能量平台现象，在能量误差、ansatz规模和测量成本上均优于基线算法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高效、鲁棒的ansatz构建范式，有助于推动大规模VQE在量子化学领域的开发与实际部署。

Abstract: Designing compact ansätze in Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) is crucial for solving energetic problems of practical molecules on near-term quantum devices. However, existing Adaptive Derivative-Assembled Pseudo-Trotter (ADAPT) ansätze face two challenges: improper operator selection and accumulation of degraded operators. In this paper, we propose the Hamiltonian-Aware (HA) ADAPT-VQE algorithm to address these issues. First, we establish a novel excitation operator selection criterion. It breaks the local constraint of existing criteria by incorporating Hamiltonian information, prioritizes physically meaningful excitation operators, and incurs no extra classical or quantum computational overhead. Furthermore, we develop a problem-adaptive method for discriminating and pruning redundant excitation operators stemming from improper selection and inevitable degradation. This method balances redundant operator pruning and convergence guarantee, and is applicable to ansätze with arbitrary scales. Systematic numerical experiments on typical strongly correlated molecular systems demonstrate that our HA-ADAPT-VQE avoids energy plateaus and outperforms baseline algorithms in terms of energy error, ansatz size, and measurement cost. This work offers an efficient, robust ansatz construction paradigm, facilitating the development and practical deployment of large-scale VQE in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [179] [Robust Pretty Good Measurement via Hybrid Classical-Quantum Pseudoinverse Approximation and Circuit-Level Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13150)
*Bikash K. Behera,Andrés Camilo Granda Arango,Giuseppe Sergioli,Roberto Giuntini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的数值鲁棒PGM方法，通过阈值正则化替代标准逆平方根，并结合块编码和谱变换技术构建混合经典-量子框架，在病态和退化场景下实现稳定量子态判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PGM在测量算子奇异或病态时数值不稳定，实际实现受限，亟需一种鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 采用Moore-Penrose伪逆替代标准逆平方根，引入阈值正则化处理谱奇异问题；构建混合经典-量子框架，经典端进行伪逆谱预处理，量子端结合块编码和谱变换实现电路；引入支持感知机制处理秩亏情形，并采用模糊幅度放大提升电路成功概率。

Result: 数值与电路仿真显示理论预测与量子电路输出高度一致；在合成和真实数据集（含病态、退化场景）上，标准PGM数值不稳定时本方法仍保持稳定的判别性能。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的混合经典-量子鲁棒量子态判别框架，将伪逆感知的测量设计扩展到电路级PGM实现中。

Abstract: Pretty Good Measurement (PGM) is a near-optimal strategy for quantum state discrimination, but its practical realization becomes unstable when the ensemble operator is singular or ill-conditioned. We introduce a numerically robust PGM formulation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse, replacing the standard inverse square root with a threshold-regularized variant that remains well-defined across different spectral regimes. We develop a hybrid classical-quantum framework that combines pseudoinverse-based spectral preprocessing with quantum circuit realizations using block-encoding and spectral-transformation techniques. The framework incorporates support awareness, yielding physically meaningful measurement operators even in rank-deficient cases, and employs oblivious amplitude amplification to improve circuit-level success probabilities. Extensive numerical and circuit-level simulations show close agreement between theoretical predictions and quantum circuit outputs. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets, including ill-conditioned and degenerate scenarios, demonstrate stable discrimination performance where standard PGM becomes numerically unstable. The results establish a practical hybrid classical-quantum framework for robust quantum state discrimination and extend previous circuit-based implementations of the PGM testing stage toward pseudoinverse-aware measurement design.

</details>


### [180] [Interference of critical dynamics associated with zero modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13200)
*Zhi-Han Zhang,Han-Chuan Kou,Peng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Creutz梯子中与零模相关的临界动力学干涉（ICDZM），通过闭合淬灭路径依次穿过两个临界点，发现丰富的ICDZM干涉图案依赖于淬灭路径，并证明了零模转移概率可作为拓扑零模临界动力学的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索闭合淬灭路径下拓扑零模的临界动力学干涉行为，揭示干涉图案与淬灭路径的依赖关系，并寻找可观测的物理量作为探针。

Method: 采用广义Creutz梯子模型，设计闭合淬灭路径使其依次穿过两个临界点；通过读取最终零模转移概率分析干涉图案；利用WKB分析解释干涉相位积累；通过边界粒子数偏离初始分数值的偏差来检测零模转移概率。

Result: 发现ICDZM干涉图案丰富且依赖于淬灭路径；当闭合路径连接两个拓扑非平凡相时，ICDZM图案可能消失或呈现周期加倍；零模转移概率可通过边界粒子数偏差检测；该概率同时捕获ICDZM振荡和非闭合淬灭路径中的反常缺陷产生。

Conclusion: ICDZM及相应的边缘缺陷可作为拓扑零模临界动力学的有效探针。

Abstract: We study the interference of critical dynamics associated with zero modes (ICDZM) in the generalized Creutz ladders using closed quench paths that pass through two critical points successively. By reading out the final zero-mode transfer probability, we find rich ICDZM interference patterns dependent on the quench path. In particular, when the closed path links two topologically nontrivial phases, the ICDZM pattern may either vanish or exhibit period doubling. Within the framework of WKB analysis, this phenomenon is well clarified by the interference phase accumulated in the quench procedure. We also demonstrate that the zero-mode transfer probability can be detected by the deviation of the boundary particle number from its initial fractional value, which arises from the blending of bulk modes in the critical dynamics. As an edge defect, the zero-mode transfer probability captures both the ICDZM oscillation and the known anomalous defect production in a non-closed quench path. These results identify ICDZM and the corresponding edge defect as probes for critical dynamics associated with topological zero modes.

</details>


### [181] [Achieving Heisenberg limit under noisy conditions with quantum Zeno dynamics and dynamical decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13205)
*Ke Zeng,Bakmou Lahcen,Yu Jiang,Kok Chuan Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子Zeno动力学(QZD)和动力学解耦(DD)在量子计量学中抑制噪声并达到海森堡极限(HL)的充要条件，证明了在马尔可夫 regime 下QZD/DD在某些场景中比量子纠错(QEC)更有效，并展示了两种技术的组合可使不完美的QZD和DD策略达到HL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子Zeno动力学和动力学解耦在量子计量学中能否抑制噪声以及能否达到海森堡极限，并给出充要条件。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别针对QZD和DD推导噪声抑制与达到HL的充要条件；在马尔可夫 regime 下比较QZD/DD与QEC的效果；分析两种技术联合使用的协同效应。

Result: 给出了QZD和DD在量子计量学中实现噪声抑制和HL的充要条件；发现马尔可夫 regime 下QZD/DD在某些场景中优于当前QEC方法；证明QZD与DD的组合可使各自不完美的策略达到HL。

Conclusion: QZD和DD在量子计量学中具有重要的实用价值，两者结合可克服各自的局限性，为实际高精度量子测量提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Quantum Zeno dynamics (QZD) and dynamical decoupling (DD) are useful tools that enable the effective suppression of noise in quantum systems. We consider the problem of when (i) noise can be suppressed and (ii) Heisenberg limit (HL) can be achieved in quantum metrology, and prove necessary and sufficient conditions for when QZD and DD are useful for achieving these two goals. We also show that in the Markovian regime, there are scenarios where preventing errors using QZD/DD may enable HL to be achieved where current QEC methods may not. Finally, we demonstrate that the combination of both techniques can allow individually imperfect QZD and DD strategies to saturate HL.

</details>


### [182] [Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA for Joint Anomaly-Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13244)
*Pauli Taipale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合样本-特征选择方法（而非传统的先特征后样本的序列选择），并引入耦合分组XY-QAOA算法来解决固定预算下的耦合约束优化问题。在稀疏IBM Heron R3基准上，硬件感知实现相比Qiskit优化等级3减少了45.9%-61.3%的电路深度和2.6%-5.2%的双量子比特门，实现了64量子比特(p=2)和36量子比特(p=3)的最大宽度-深度配置，且20量子比特(p=5)运行保留63%有效样本。实验表明联合选择对校准误差敏感度更低，固定角度运行优于随机可行采样，温启动缩小与严格可行经典参考的差距57.5%-80.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列特征优先选择方法在固定预算下选择异常样本和解释性特征时，会忽略特征效用取决于所选样本的情况，尤其是在参考数据有限、存在噪声或漂移时。作者旨在将任务形式化为固定计数下的联合样本-特征选择，以解决耦合约束优化问题，并克服校准误差敏感度随样本数量线性增长的问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为固定预算下的联合样本-特征选择；分析校准误差敏感度在特征优先排序与联合选择中的差异；提出Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA（约束保持分组角度变体）用于求解该优化问题；采用硬件感知实现以降低电路深度和双量子比特门数量；引入温启动和近预算修复策略。

Result: 在稀疏IBM Heron R3基准上，电路深度降低45.9%-61.3%，双量子比特门减少2.6%-5.2%；实现64量子比特(p=2)和36量子比特(p=3)的最大已知宽度-深度配置；20量子比特(p=5)保留63%有效样本；固定角度运行在36-64量子比特范围内能量低于随机可行采样；温启动将差距缩小57.5%-80.5%；近预算修复在36量子比特处匹配稀疏经典参考。

Conclusion: 联合样本-特征选择在固定预算下优于序列方法，对校准误差的敏感度更低；所提出的耦合分组XY-QAOA算法在硬件可实现性、电路深度和门数优化方面表现优异；问题结构化角度分组优于相同深度的XY-QAOA和匹配参数随机化控制；结果支持在测试范围内进行校准联合选择和约束混合器硬件执行。

Abstract: Selecting anomalous samples and explanatory features under fixed budgets defines a coupled constrained-optimization problem. Sequential feature-first selection ranks features before choosing samples, which can overlook features whose utility depends on which samples are selected, especially when scores are calibrated from reference data that may be limited, noisy, or drifting. We instead formulate the task as joint sample-feature selection under the same fixed counts. In the analyzed formal model, calibration-error sensitivity grows linearly with the number of samples for feature-first ordering but stays constant for joint selection. We introduce Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA, a constraint-preserving grouped-angle variant for the resulting optimization problem. On matched sparse IBM Heron R3 benchmarks, a hardware-aware implementation reduces circuit depth by 45.9%-61.3% and two-qubit gates by 2.6%-5.2% relative to Qiskit optimization level 3 on the CZ-basis target. It enables, to our knowledge, the largest reported width-depth configurations for constraint-preserving bipartite-selection QAOA hardware executions with feasible-sector retention: 64 qubits at p=2 and 36 qubits at p=3. The 20-qubit p=5 runs retain 63% valid samples. Across 36-64 qubits, fixed-angle runs yield lower-energy feasible samples than matched random-feasible sampling. Warm starts reduce the gap to strict-feasible classical references by 57.5%-80.5%, and near-budget repair matches the sparse classical reference at 36 qubits. Benchmarks show gains in balanced fixed-budget regimes, and noiseless simulations show that problem-structured angle grouping improves over same-depth XY-QAOA and matched-parameter, type-preserving randomization controls. Overall, the results support calibrated joint selection and hardware-realizable constrained-mixer execution in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [183] [Kubo-Martin-Schwinger conditions for non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13251)
*Chen Lan,Luyao Ma,Hao Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非厄米哈密顿量在实谱和双正交本征系统下KMS热平衡条件的扩展，核心结果表明：对于可对角化且具有实谱的H，双正交Gibbs泛函满足正定性当且仅当H是准厄米的。通过Riesz表示定理直接从特征投影构造度量η，无需预先选择η，提供了Mostafazadeh-Scholtz框架之外的准厄米性无度量判据。在完全准厄米假设下，证明了η-Gibbs态满足所有三条解析KMS条件，并揭示了与等谱厄米伴侣的Gibbs态的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将KMS热平衡条件从标准厄米量子体系拓展到具有实谱的非厄米哈密顿量，探究准厄米性在热力学框架中的核心地位，并解决非厄米系统中热平衡态的定义问题。

Method: 通过三条互补路径进行系统分析：利用Riesz表示定理从双正交Gibbs泛函的特征投影构造度量η；采用Hadamard三线定理和Bari的Riesz基定理证明η-Gibbs态的KMS条件；分析奇异点（exceptional points）和复谱下的失效模式，并与Fagnola-Umanità量子细致平衡条件进行对比。

Result: (1) 双正交Gibbs泛函对所有A满足正定性当且仅当H是准厄米的；(2) 在准厄米假设下，η-Gibbs态满足全部KMS条件；(3) 证明了该结果无法通过相似变换从厄米理论简单推导，因为当[η,h]≠0时转移态不同于等谱厄米伴侣的Gibbs态；(4) 明确了与完整Haag-Hugenholtz-Winnink C*-代数框架之间的差距。

Conclusion: 建立了非厄米哈密顿量热力学的形式化基础，准厄米性被表征为热力学正定性的充要条件；为非厄米量子统计力学和开放量子系统热化问题提供了理论工具，并指出了向代数量子场论框架推广的局限性。

Abstract: We investigate the extension of the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) thermal equilibrium condition to non-Hermitian Hamiltonians with real spectra and biorthogonal eigensystems, providing a systematic analysis through three complementary routes. Our central result is a thermodynamic characterisation of quasi-Hermiticity: for $H \in M_d(\mathbb{C})$ diagonalisable with real spectrum, the biorthogonal Gibbs functional $ω_{\rm{bi}}(A) = Z_{\rm{bi}}^{-1} \sum_n e^{-βE_n}\langleφ_n|A|ψ_n\rangle$ satisfies $ω_{\rm{bi}}(A^†A) \geq 0$ for all $A$ if and only if $H$ is quasi-Hermitian. The proof constructs the metric $η$ directly from the eigenprojectors of $ω_{\rm{bi}}$ via the Riesz representation theorem, with no prior choice of $η$, providing a metric-free certificate of quasi-Hermiticity outside the Mostafazadeh--Scholtz framework. Under the full quasi-Hermitian hypothesis, we prove that the $η$-Gibbs state $ω_η(A) = Z_η^{-1}\, \rm{Tr}[ηe^{-βH}A]$ satisfies all three analytic KMS conditions, using the Hadamard three-line theorem and Bari's theorem on Riesz bases. The result is non-trivial: the transported state $\hatω(X) = \rm{Tr}[e^{-βh}Xη]/Z_η$ differs from the Gibbs state of the isospectral Hermitian partner $h = η^{1/2}Hη^{-1/2}$ whenever $[η,h]\neq 0$, so the KMS property cannot be deduced from the Hermitian theory by similarity. The gap between this result and the full Haag--Hugenholtz--Winnink $C^*$-algebraic framework is identified. Failure modes at exceptional points and for complex spectra are analysed, and the relation to the Fagnola--Umanità quantum detailed balance condition for open systems is discussed.

</details>


### [184] [Where a Quantum Reservoir Works: A Transferable Operating Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13284)
*Markus Baumann,Itamar Fink,Johannes Wittmann,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien,Jonas Stein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过映射耗散量子储层计算在三个物理控制参数（输入驱动强度、相邻量子比特耦合强度、耗散率）下的性能表现，发现存在一个唯一的、定义良好的操作区域，该区域在不同任务和初始化条件下表现优异，可通过简单记忆诊断廉价定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子储层计算中，量子动力学固定不变，仅训练线性读出层，因此需要在训练前就选定合适的动力学参数。但如何在不优化量子系统本身的情况下，确定固定量子系统在控制空间中何处能实现良好学习性能，仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 对耗散量子储层计算系统，系统性地在三个关键物理控制参数（输入驱动强度、相邻量子比特耦合强度、耗散率）构成的控制空间中映射性能表现，并分析该性能区域在不同任务、不同储备层初始化、不同架构下的迁移性，以及通过消融实验验证其机制基础。

Result: 良好的性能集中在一个单一、定义明确的控制空间操作区域；该区域可跨任务和储备层初始化迁移；相同的记忆定义区域在架构变化下保持稳定；该区域具有可解释的机械基础，移除任何产生它的机制都会导致区域消失；该区域可在运行任何任务前通过简单的记忆诊断廉价定位。

Conclusion: 在耗散量子储层计算中，存在一个通用的最优操作区域，该区域由系统的记忆特性决定，可在无任务信息的情况下提前定位，为固定量子系统的控制参数选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: In quantum reservoir computing, a fixed quantum system transforms an input signal, while learning reduces to training a simple linear readout on its measured outputs. Since the quantum dynamics themselves are never optimized, the method is well suited to today's hardware. Yet these dynamics must still be chosen carefully, because their settings remain fixed throughout training and inference. It therefore remains an open question where, in its control space, a fixed quantum system learns well. We address this question for a dissipative reservoir by mapping performance over three central physical controls: the strength of the input drive, the coupling between neighboring qubits, and the rate of dissipation. Good performance concentrates in a single, well-defined operating region of this control space. This region transfers across tasks and reservoir initializations, and the same memory-defined regime persists under architectural changes. It is also mechanistically grounded, since it disappears whenever any of the mechanisms that create it is removed. Finally, the region can be located cheaply before any task is run, using a simple memory diagnostic.

</details>


### [185] [Driven-dissipative entanglement of distant giant atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13375)
*Aziza Almanakly,Ariadna Soro,Alejandro Vivas-Viaña,Beatriz Yankelevich,Caspar Groiseau,David Pahl,Junyoung An,Gabriel Cutter,Michael E. Gingras,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Hannah Stickler,Renée DePéncier Piñero,Mollie E. Schwartz,Kyle Serniak,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,Anton Frisk Kockum,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过超导巨原子系统，利用驱动-耗散协议在波导中生成并保护远程纠缠，实现了保真度F=0.89±0.02的贝尔态，证明了巨原子的驱动耗散是量子网络中分发纠缠的可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的纠缠生成方案多依赖相干可逆相互作用，需要精确校准的脉冲；而驱动-耗散协议虽能稳定纠缠，但耗散本身会在稳定协议结束后限制纠缠的实用性。因此，需要一种既能生成纠缠又能有效保护纠缠的方案。

Method: 构建了两个顺序耦合到波导的超导巨原子系统，通过耦合点间的干涉实现可调谐的个体耗散和关联耗散。利用连续波驱动通过波导激发关联耗散以生成远程纠缠，随后原位调谐量子比特频率以抑制个体耗散，从而保护纠缠。

Result: 实现了贝尔态保真度F=0.89±0.02的远程纠缠。

Conclusion: 巨原子的驱动耗散是实现量子网络中纠缠分发的可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum interconnects distribute entanglement via controlled light-matter interactions for quantum computing and sensing applications. Many entanglement generation schemes use coherent, reversible interactions that require precisely calibrated pulses to execute. In contrast, driven-dissipative protocols use a continuous-wave drive in the presence of correlated dissipation to stabilize entanglement in protected (dark) states. However, the same dissipation that generates the entanglement also limits its utility once the stabilization protocol ends. Here, we engineer a superconducting system of two giant artificial atoms coupled sequentially to a waveguide, with tunable individual and correlated dissipation enabled by interference between coupling points. Continuously driving the atoms through the waveguide exploits correlated dissipation to generate remote entanglement. We then tune the qubit frequencies in situ to suppress individual dissipation and thereby preserve the entanglement, achieving a Bell-state fidelity F = 0.89 +/- 0.02. This demonstration indicates that the driven dissipation of giant atoms is a viable approach for distributing entanglement across quantum networks.

</details>


### [186] [Steady-State Noise Signatures of Lindbladian Exceptional Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13377)
*Shihang Pan,Gianmichele Blasi,Géraldine Haack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了可以通过稳态电流噪声来探测开放量子系统中Lindblad算符的奇异点（EPs），并推导了电流关联函数的一般表达式，通过两个相互作用的量子比特与两个热库耦合的实例展示了过阻尼、欠阻尼和临界态在噪声中的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Lindblad奇异点的特征主要在有限时间观测（如瞬态电流）中被识别，稳态平均电流无法直接反映EP结构。作者希望证明稳态下的电流噪声可以用于探测这些奇异点。

Method: 在Lindblad主方程框架下推导了电流关联函数的一般表达式，分析EP如何影响其时延依赖行为；以两个相互作用的量子比特耦合到两个热库为范例进行数值验证。

Result: 稳态电流噪声能够清晰区分过阻尼、欠阻尼和临界态，稳态噪声随延迟时间的变化可以反映Lindblad EPs的特征。

Conclusion: 电流关联函数可以作为开放量子系统中Lindblad奇异点的稳态探针。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are non-Hermitian degeneracies at which two or more eigenvalues and their corresponding eigenvectors coalesce. In open quantum systems, exceptional points can arise in the Lindbladian governing the dissipative dynamics. Their signatures have so far been mainly identified in finite-time observables, such as transient currents, while steady-state average currents generally provide no direct evidence of the underlying exceptional-point structure. In this work, we demonstrate that signatures of Lindbladian EPs can nevertheless be accessed in the steady-state regime through current noise. We derive general expressions for current correlation functions within a Lindblad master-equation framework and show, in particular, how exceptional points affect their behaviour as a function of the time delay. We illustrate these results with the paradigmatic example of two interacting qubits coupled to two reservoirs, where the steady-state noise clearly distinguishes overdamped, underdamped, and critical regimes. Our results establish current correlation functions as a steady-state probe of Lindbladian EPs in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [187] [An LLM System for Autonomous Variational Quantum Circuit Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13380)
*Kenya Sakka,Wataru Mizukami,Kosuke Mitarai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型的自主智能体框架，用于在显式设计约束下进行迭代式量子电路设计，并在量子特征映射和变分量子本征求解器两个任务上取得了优于或可比于传统方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能量子电路的设计目前仍高度依赖人类专家经验，缺乏自动化设计方法，因此需要引入AI系统来实现自主、高效的量子电路设计。

Method: 提出包含七个组件（探索、生成、讨论、验证、存储、评估、评审）的闭环智能体框架，结合网络知识获取、文献支持的评阅、可执行代码生成和实验反馈，利用LLM驱动迭代优化。

Result: 在量子机器学习图像分类任务中，生成的最佳特征映射优于代表性量子特征映射，并在大比特数下超越经典径向基函数核；在量子化学的七种分子基态估计中，生成的ansatz在满足约束条件下达到与化学启发式和硬件高效构造相当的精度。

Conclusion: 证明了LLM驱动的智能体系统可作为量子电路自动设计的可行范式，展示了AI系统参与跨学科迭代式科学优化流程的能力。

Abstract: The design of high performing quantum circuits remains largely dependent on human expertise. We introduce an autonomous agentic framework that employs large language models (LLMs) to conduct iterative quantum circuit designs under explicit design constraints. Our system integrates seven components: Exploration, Generation, Discussion, Validation, Storage, Evaluation, and Review. These components form a closed-loop workflow that combines web-based knowledge acquisition, literature-grounded critique, executable code generation, and experimental feedback. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: quantum feature map construction for quantum machine learning and ansatz generation for variational quantum eigensolver applications in quantum chemistry. In image classification benchmarks, the best generated feature map outperforms representative quantum feature maps and, when scaled to larger qubit counts, surpasses the classical radial basis function kernel. In molecular ground state estimation across seven molecules, the generated ansatz attains competitive accuracy with widely used chemically inspired and hardware-efficient constructions while satisfying the imposed scaling constraints. These results establish LLM driven agentic system as a viable paradigm for automated quantum circuit design and illustrate how AI systems can participate in iterative scientific optimization workflows across scientific domains.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond the Unruh vacuum: multi-time correlations in black hole collapse and evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13383)
*Konstantinos Xenos,Charis Anastopoulos,Andreas F. Terzis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过二维引力塌缩与蒸发模型证明，黑洞霍金辐射中多时间量子场关联可携带塌缩前物质信息，而仅基于单时间观测量的信息佯谬表述是不完备的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息佯谬源于霍金辐射的热本质似乎抹去了塌缩物质的信息，但热性仅约束单时间观测量，多时间量子关联结构尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用二维引力塌缩与蒸发模型，分析多时间量子场关联函数，并与Unruh真空进行比较。

Result: 晚期多时间关联不能完全由Unruh真空重现，其中包含依赖于塌缩前状态参数的贡献，尽管渐近辐射具有热性。

Conclusion: 多时间关联是霍金辐射中信息的载体，仅基于单时间观测量的信息佯谬表述是不完备的。

Abstract: The black hole information paradox originates from the thermal character of Hawking radiation, which appears to erase information about the collapsing matter. However, thermality constrains only observables defined at a single time and leaves the structure of temporal quantum correlations largely unexplored. Here we show that multi-time quantum-field correlations provide a concrete mechanism for the survival of pre-collapse information in black hole evaporation. Using a two-dimensional model of gravitational collapse and evaporation, we demonstrate that late-time multi-time correlations are not fully reproduced by the Unruh vacuum. In particular, they contain a contribution that depends explicitly on parameters characterizing the pre-collapse state, despite the thermal character of the asymptotic radiation. Our results identify measurable multi-time correlations as carriers of information in Hawking radiation and suggest that formulations of the black hole information paradox based solely on single-time observables are incomplete.

</details>


### [189] [Representation-Induced Symmetry Trapping in Adaptive Variational Quantum Simulations of Multi-Reference Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13387)
*Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过系统分析非对称分子在不同费米子-量子比特映射（Bravyi-Kitaev变换）下的优化困境，本文揭示了点群对称性保护对变分量子化学算法可训练性的关键作用，并提出了一种协方差驱动的自适应 shot 分配滤波器，用于动态诊断和终止因表示诱导的平台态，从而在早期容错架构上实现资源高效的深度变分算法执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自适应量子化学算法在多参考静态关联下的可训练性，特别是表示拓扑与分子几何如何交织影响优化景观，并揭示当前忽略的费米子-量子比特映射表示对可训练性的关键作用。

Method: 系统评估自旋守恒SUSD算子池在非对称LiH、对称BeH2和非对称H2O的高度拉伸构型（2倍平衡键长）下的表现，对比对称拉伸基线；引入协方差驱动的自适应shot分配滤波器，基于梯度精度阈值epsilon动态诊断并终止表示诱导的平缓态。

Result: 非对称畸变下Bravyi-Kitaev变换的非局域映射约束导致优化陷阱效应（编码锁定的贫瘠高原危机）；点群对称性保护可结构化地保护优化景观；表示映射是维持景观可训练性的关键因素；提出的自适应shot分配滤波器能主动剪枝死对称通道并触发自动电路终止序列。

Conclusion: 对称性限制对变分量子算法而言必要但不充分，必须考虑底层费米子-量子比特映射表示的影响；结合代数测量复用与拓扑感知统计滤波的策略，为早期容错架构上的深度变分算法执行提供了有前景的资源高效方案。

Abstract: Evaluating the trainability of adaptive quantum chemistry algorithms under multi-reference static correlation requires understanding how representation topologies intertwine with molecular geometry. We systematically expose a deep physical dependence on point-group symmetry by evaluating a spin-conserved SUSD operator pool across highly stretched configurations (2 x Re) of asymmetric LiH, symmetric BeH2, and asymmetric H2O. Under asymmetric distortions, the non-local mapping constraints of the Bravyi-Kitaev transformation create an optimization trapping effect--an encodement-locked manifestation of the broader barren plateau crisis. Crucially, by comparing these to the symmetrical stretching baseline of BeH2, we demonstrate that the preservation of point-group symmetry structurally protects the optimization landscape, proving that ansatz symmetry restrictions are necessary but insufficient without accounting for the underlying fermion-to-qubit representation. While current methods rely on numerical pruning to throttle pool sizes, our structural approach establishes that the mapping representation remains a critical factor in maintaining landscape trainability. Furthermore, exploiting structural overlap within our pool, we introduce a covariance-driven, adaptive shot-allocation filter. Diverging from static energy-variance minimization frameworks, our allocation engine operates as a dynamic runtime diagnostic tool. By continuously monitoring the gradient precision threshold epsilon, it aggressively prunes dead symmetry channels and triggers an automated circuit-termination sequence upon detecting representation-induced flat-lined states (dE/dtheta approx 0). This integration of algebraic measurement reuse with topology-aware statistical filtering provides a promising, resource-efficient strategy for executing deep variational algorithms on early fault-tolerant architectures.

</details>


### [190] [Characterizing the functional role of quantum coherence in energy transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13404)
*Hallmann Óskar Gestsson,Alexandra Olaya-Castro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Nakajima-Zwanzig投影算子推导出一般记忆核恒等式，在特征能量基下定量表征量子相干性对能量转移广义速率的影响，并通过二聚体-结构化声子浴模型展示了量子相干性对能量转移的调制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干性被认为在开放量子系统的激发能量转移中发挥作用，但缺乏定量评估其影响的严格方法。

Method: 利用Nakajima-Zwanzig投影算子推导一般记忆核恒等式，定量表征特征能量基下相干性对能量转移广义速率的影响。

Result: 推导出记忆核恒等式，能够表征并量化特征能量基下相干性对能量转移广义速率的影响，并在二聚体-结构化声子浴模型中展示了量子相干性对能量转移的调制作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的记忆核恒等式框架为定量评估量子相干性在能量转移过程中的影响提供了严格方法，揭示了相干性对能量转移的调制机制。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is understood to play a role in excitation energy transfer in open quantum systems, yet a quantitative approach to assessing its influence on the transfer process is still missing. Using Nakajima-Zwanzig projection operators, we derive a general memory kernel identity that enables us to characterize and quantify the impact of coherence in the eigenenergy basis on a generalized rate of energy transfer. Applying our approach to the electronic dynamics of a dimer coupled to a structured phonon bath, we demonstrate how quantum coherence acts to modulate energy transfer.

</details>


### [191] [Kerr-induced nonreciprocal transparency and group delay in a hybrid cavity magnomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13412)
*M. Amghar,Noura Chabar,Mohamed Amazioug,Reem Altuijri,Ameenah N. Al-Ahmadi,Abdel-Haleem Abdel-Aty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种在混合腔磁力机械系统中实现非互易透明、Fano共振和慢/快光的方案，利用磁子克尔非线性产生方向依赖的频率位移和干涉路径调控，实现了可控的非互易传输和群延迟切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合腔磁力机械系统中利用磁子克尔非线性实现非互易透明、Fano共振和慢/快光，探索克尔工程化磁力机械系统在非互易微波光子学和量子信息技术中的应用潜力。

Method: 构建含有两个YIG球体和机械谐振器的混合腔磁力机械系统，利用磁子克尔非线性诱导方向依赖的频率位移，改变腔光子、磁子和声子之间的干涉路径。通过分析混合耦合强度和耗散通道，确定最大化非互易响应的参数区域。

Result: 系统支持多个由磁子和磁力机械诱导干涉过程产生的透明窗口；克尔相互作用重塑透明特征，产生不对称Fano线型和可控非互易传输；色散表现出显著的方向不对称性，导致相反传播方向上的群延迟巨大差异，实现慢光和快光之间的可逆切换。

Conclusion: 克尔工程化磁力机械系统为集成非互易微波光子学和量子信息技术提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: We propose a scheme for realizing nonreciprocal transparency, Fano resonances, and slow/fast light in a hybrid cavity magnomechanical system containing two YIG spheres and a mechanical resonator. The nonreciprocal behavior originates from the magnon Kerr nonlinearity, which induces direction-dependent frequency shifts and modifies the interference pathways among cavity photons, magnons, and phonons. We show that the hybrid system supports multiple transparency windows arising from magnon- and magnomechanical-induced interference processes. The Kerr interaction strongly reshapes these transparency features, producing asymmetric Fano line shapes and enabling controllable nonreciprocal transmission. Furthermore, the associated dispersion exhibits pronounced directional asymmetry, leading to giant differences in the group delay for opposite propagation directions and allowing reversible switching between slow- and fast-light regimes. We investigate the roles of hybrid coupling strengths and dissipation channels and identify parameter regimes where the nonreciprocal response is maximized. These findings establish Kerr-engineered magnomechanical systems as promising platforms for integrated nonreciprocal microwave photonics and quantum information technologies.

</details>


### [192] [Foundations of Practical Quantum Advantage in Quantum-Informed Machine Learning for Predicting Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13422)
*Maida Wang,Xiao Xue,Minh Chung,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为混沌动力系统中的量子-informed机器学习建立了理论基础，提出了一族k阶量子统计先验（Q-Priors），证明了两阶段量子优势：表示阶段利用叠加和纠缠紧凑存储不变度量的非因子化空间相关性；提取阶段通过联合Bell测量实现与量子比特数无关的副本对计数，而自适应单副本协议需要指数级副本，形成可证明的量子-经典分离。通过湍流通道流和ERA5中期天气预报两个案例验证了机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为混沌动力系统中的量子-informed机器学习建立理论化的量子优势机制，证明量子计算在表示和提取不变度量统计特征方面相较于经典方法的根本性优势，并寻找在容错量子硬件实现前的实用量子优势候选路径。

Method: 构建一族k索引的高阶量子统计先验（Q-Priors），将不变度量的k点边际编码在n_q=kq个量子比特上；在表示阶段利用叠加和纠缠紧凑存储非因子化空间相关性；在提取阶段采用双副本联合Bell测量估计任意事后Pauli泛函，并与自适应单副本协议进行复制测量复杂度对比；在IQM超导处理器上进行仿真和实验验证，并通过湍流通道流和ERA5再分析天气预报两个案例进行应用测试。

Result: 双副本联合Bell测量的副本对计数与n_q无关，而自适应单副本协议需要Ω(2^(n_q))个副本，形成可证明的量子-经典分离；在天气预报案例中，k≤2的对角Q-Prior引导Koopman展开，在48-240小时提前时间内将异常相关技巧提升10-39%，并减少了长时间展开向静态平均场的崩溃。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段量子优势机制在互补层面满足了实用量子优势定义的两个条件，确立了在容错硬件实现前获取实用量子优势的候选路径。

Abstract: We develop theoretical foundations for a practical quantum-advantage mechanism in quantum-informed machine learning for chaotic dynamical systems. A family of k-indexed higher-order quantum statistical priors (Q-Priors) hosts the k-point marginal of the invariant measure on n_q = kq qubits, extending the single-site construction of prior work. We prove a two-stage advantage. In the representation stage, superposition and entanglement compactly store non-factorisable spatial correlations of the invariant measure on n_q qubits. In the extraction stage, joint Bell measurements on two copies estimate any post hoc Pauli functional with a copy-pair count independent of n_q, whereas any adaptive single-copy protocol for the corresponding full-Pauli read-out requires Omega(2^(n_q)) copies; this is a provable quantum-classical separation in copy-measurement complexity. The two-copy read-out is realised in simulation and on IQM superconducting processors. Two case studies instantiate the mechanism in workflows of independent scientific value: a turbulent channel-flow study in which the two-copy read-out yields a named non-diagonal correlator of the invariant measure (the velocity-direction coherence), and a medium-range weather forecasting workflow on the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts ERA5 reanalysis in which the diagonal k <= 2 Q-Prior steers a Koopman rollout, improves anomaly-correlation skill by 10-39% across 48-240 h lead times, and reduces the long-horizon collapse of rollouts onto a static mean field. The two conditions of our practical-advantage definition are met at complementary levels, identifying a candidate route to practical quantum advantage before fault-tolerant hardware.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum optical photoelectron interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13447)
*Jonathan Dubois,Viviane Cotte,Richard Taïeb,Camille Lévêque,Jérémie Caillat,Pranshu Dave,Pascal Salières,David Bresteau,Charles Bourassin-Bouchet,Anne L'Huillier,David Busto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子光场驱动多光子过程的理论框架，揭示了光子统计特性（自相关和互相关函数）直接映射到光电子能谱中。以RABBIT方法为例，分析了量子光场统计特性对边带信号振幅、对比度和相位的影响，并建立了量子光RABBIT光谱学的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用经典光场描述，缺乏对量子光场统计特性如何影响多光子过程及光电子观测量的系统性理论理解。

Method: 构建量子光场驱动多光子过程的理论框架，推导光子统计（自相关/互相关函数）与光电子能谱的直接映射关系；以RABBIT为具体案例，分析多种量子配置（相关红外与谐波模式、非经典谐波统计等）；并与经典谐波+压缩相干态红外场的数值模拟进行对比验证。

Result: 光子统计特性直接映射到光电子能谱；边带信号的振幅、对比度和相位揭示了光的量子性质；在经典谐波与压缩相干态红外场的场景下，解析理论与数值模拟高度吻合。

Conclusion: 经典与量子关联的相互作用决定了光发射过程的相干性，为阿秒科学的量子光学基础提供了新视角。

Abstract: We present a general theoretical framework for multiphoton processes driven by quantum light fields, establishing a direct link between photon statistics and photoelectron observables. Our results show that the autocorrelation and cross-correlation functions, which quantify the underlying photon statistics, are directly mapped onto the resulting photoelectron spectra. Although our framework is broadly applicable, we demonstrate specifically in the example of reconstruction of attosecond beating by interference of two-photon transitions (RABBIT) the influence of the light statistical properties. In this approach, the amplitude, contrast and phase of the oscillations of the sideband signal as a function of pump-probe delay reveal the quantum nature of light. We analyze these observables across several quantum configurations, including correlated infrared and harmonic modes, as well as the uncorrelated case with non-classical harmonic statistics, thereby establishing a general framework for quantum-light RABBIT spectroscopy. We compare the analytical theory with numerical simulations for the case of classical harmonics and an infrared field in a squeezed coherent state, obtaining excellent agreement. Our results reveal how the interplay between classical and quantum correlations dictates the coherence of the photoemission process, providing a new window into the quantum-optical foundations of attosecond science.

</details>


### [194] [Invariant Measures and Weak-Magic-Injection Asymptotics in Random Monitored Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13470)
*Guocheng Zhen,Xuanrong Yang,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为随机监控量子电路的动力学建立了严格的数学分析框架，证明了平稳律的存在唯一性，并解析求解了弱魔法注入极限下稳态魔法的渐近行为，揭示了不同资源度量（Gross-Wigner mana与2-稳定子Rényi熵）具有不同的渐近幂次（线性vs二次），反映了各自局域几何的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对监控量子电路的理解主要依赖数值模拟和唯象描述，缺乏严格的动力学理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，为随机监控量子动力学提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 研究由随机Clifford驱动的监控量子电路这类系统。首先证明平稳律的存在唯一性（遍历描述），然后在弱魔法注入极限下求解稳态魔法的领先渐近行为，并通过切空间描述揭示不同资源度量的渐近差异。

Result: 1) 证明了随机监控量子电路平稳律的存在唯一性；2) 解析得到了弱魔法注入极限下稳态魔法的领先渐近行为；3) 发现奇数素数局域维度下Gross-Wigner mana稳态为线性渐近，而qubit系统中2-稳定子Rényi熵稳态为二次渐近。

Conclusion: 本文首次为随机监控量子电路建立了平稳遍历动力学分析框架，解析证明了不同非稳定子资源度量具有不同的渐近行为，这源于它们靠近稳定子层时的局域几何结构差异。

Abstract: Monitored quantum circuits provide a natural setting in which scrambling, measurements, and measurement-conditioned updates compete within a stochastic many-body dynamics. From the viewpoint of nonstabilizer resource theory, this competition is especially relevant because Clifford-compatible operations preserve the stabilizer structure, while weak non-Clifford perturbations inject magic resource. Most of the existing understanding of monitored quantum circuits has been shaped by numerical simulations and phenomenological descriptions, while a rigorous dynamics theory remains less developed. In this paper, we address this gap by developing an analytical framework which lays a rigorous mathematical foundation for the study of random monitored quantum dynamics. Specifically, we study a class of monitored quantum circuits driven by random Clifford. We prove the existence and uniqueness of the stationary law, which gives an ergodic description of the long-time dynamics. We then resolve the leading asymptotics of steady magic in the weak-magic-injection limit. This tangent description makes the contrast between resource measures transparent: in odd-prime local dimension, the steady Gross--Wigner mana has a linear leading asymptotic, whereas in qubit systems the steady 2-stabilizer Rényi entropy has a quadratic leading asymptotic. These different powers reflect the distinct local geometries of the two resource measures near the stabilizer layer. In this way, this work develops an analytical framework that first establishes the stationary ergodic dynamics of random monitored quantum circuits.

</details>


### [195] [Observation of Non-Gaussian Magnon Dynamics in a Two-Dimensional Long-Range XY Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13499)
*S. -A. Guo,J. -Y. Tan,J. Ye,Y. Jiang,L. Zhang,Y. -X. Chen,H. -J. Chen,H. -Y. Hu,W. -X. Guo,B. -X. Qi,L. He,Z. -C. Zhou,Y. -K. Wu,L. -M. Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维XY模型（具有长程和空间结构相互作用）的 trapped ion 量子模拟器中，通过调控磁子激发密度，观测了自旋关联从高斯动力学到非高斯动力学的交叉过渡，并在校准误差无关的方式下验证了非高斯行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯的高阶自旋关联演化是量子多体系统的重要特征，但实际中退相干、统计涨落和实验参数校准误差都会阻碍对非高斯动力学的观测，因此需要一种能够排除这些干扰的验证方法。

Method: 使用 trapped ion 量子模拟器实现二维XY模型（长程、空间结构化相互作用）；制备不同初始密度的磁子激发；通过比较单自旋可观测量、平均场解和 Holstein-Primakoff 近似下的高阶自旋关联，以校准误差无关的方式展示非高斯行为。

Result: 成功演示了从高斯到非高斯动力学的交叉过渡，并在排除校准误差影响的情况下验证了非高斯行为。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条从经典可模拟动力学到量子优势可能出现区域的可验证路径。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian evolution of high-order spin correlations characterizes important properties of quantum many-body systems. In practice, decoherence, statistical fluctuation and miscalibration of experimental parameters all hinder the witness of non-Gaussian dynamics. Here we demonstrate the crossover between Gaussian and non-Gaussian dynamics on a two-dimensional XY model with long-range and spatially structured interaction using a trapped ion quantum simulator. We prepare different initial densities of magnon excitations and verify the dynamics of single-spin observables for the engineered Hamiltonian. Then we compare the high-order spin correlations with the mean-field solution and the Holstein-Primakoff approximation, and demonstrate the non-Gaussian behavior in a way independent of the calibration errors. Our work provides a verifiable path from classically simulatable dynamics to regimes where quantum advantage may emerge.

</details>


### [196] [A refined thermodynamic analysis of nonsecular master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13504)
*Mohamed Boubakour,Talia Szikman,Cyril Elouard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对非世俗主方程进行了系统的热力学分析，建立了统一的热力学框架，证明了系统-浴相互作用能和兰姆移位在能量平衡中的贡献，并指出非世俗主方程与世俗主方程在熵产生率上的差异源于能量本征基中非平稳相干性的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非世俗主方程（来自部分世俗近似和几何-算术近似）在系统某些跃迁频率过小无法使用完全世俗近似时能保证动力学正定性，但其稳态并非裸哈密顿量的吉布斯态。需要建立一致的热力学框架来理解这些非吉布斯稳态的热力学含义。

Method: 基于系统-环境关联的微观第二定律表达式，采用系统微扰理论保持第二定律的正定性；比较微观熵产生与基于约化系统动力学压缩性（Spohn不等式）的熵产生定义。

Result: 系统-浴相互作用能和兰姆移位参与能量平衡，非平衡时产生系统对浴的功；两种熵产生率因非平稳相干性而不同；单热浴下差异是瞬态的，无法从非吉布斯稳态中循环提取功。

Conclusion: 非世俗主方程的热力学框架可以统一建立，尽管稳态非吉布斯，但单热浴情况下无法从稳态循环提取功，澄清并完善了量子马尔可夫动力学的热力学图景。

Abstract: We present a systematic thermodynamic analysis of nonsecular master equations.
